. Therefore, we have

\|\Omega_{mm}-\widehat{\Omega}_{a,mm}\|_{2}=O_{p}\left(\frac{s+m+K}{\sqrt{n}}\right).

(55)

Then,

\displaystyle~\left\|\Psi_{n}^{-1/2}{\bm{C}}\left(\widehat{\Omega}_{a,mm}-\Omega_{mm}\right){\bm{C}}^{T}\Psi_{n}^{-1/2}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\left\|\Psi_{n}^{-1/2}{\bm{C}}\Omega_{mm}^{1/2}\Omega_{mm}^{-1/2}\left(\widehat{\Omega}_{a,mm}-\Omega_{mm}\right)\Omega_{mm}^{-1/2}\Omega_{mm}^{1/2}{\bm{C}}^{T}\Psi_{n}^{-1/2}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\left\|\Psi_{n}^{-1/2}{\bm{C}}\Omega_{mm}^{1/2}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left\|\Omega_{mm}^{-1/2}\left(\widehat{\Omega}_{a,mm}-\Omega_{mm}\right)\Omega_{mm}^{-1/2}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~1\cdot\|\Omega_{mm}^{-1/2}\|_{2}^{2}\left\|\widehat{\Omega}_{a,mm}-\Omega_{mm}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~O_{p}\left(\frac{s+m+K}{\sqrt{n}}\right),

where
\|\Omega_{mm}^{-1/2}\|_{2}^{2}=O(1)

is because the 4th condition in (A1) implies that
\lambda_{\max}(\bm{K}_{n})=O(1)
and thus
\lim\inf_{n}\lambda_{\min}(\Omega_{mm})>0
. Thus, we proved the equation (
50
). To show equation (
51
), notice that

\displaystyle~\lim\inf_{n}\lambda_{\min}\left(\Psi_{n}^{-1/2}{\bm{C}\widehat{\Omega}_{a,mm}\bm{C}^{T}}\Psi_{n}^{-1/2}\right)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle~\lim\inf_{n}\lambda_{\min}\left(\Psi_{n}^{-1/2}{\bm{C}{\Omega}_{mm}\bm{C}^{T}}\Psi_{n}^{-1/2}\right)-\lim\sup_{n}\left\|\Psi_{n}^{-1/2}{\bm{C}({\Omega}_{mm}-\widehat{\Omega}_{a,mm})\bm{C}^{T}}\Psi_{n}^{1/2}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~1-\lim\sup_{n}\left\|\Psi_{n}^{-1/2}{\bm{C}({\Omega}_{mm}-\widehat{\Omega}_{a,mm})\bm{C}^{T}}\Psi_{n}^{1/2}\right\|_{2}.

This together with (
50
) and the condition
s+m+K=o(\sqrt{n})
implies that

\lim\inf_{n}\lambda_{\min}\left(\Psi_{n}^{-1/2}{\bm{C}\widehat{\Omega}_{a,mm}\bm{C}^{T}}\Psi_{n}^{-1/2}\right)>0

(56)

with probability tending to
1
. Or equivalently,
\lambda_{\max}\left(\Psi_{n}^{1/2}({\bm{C}\widehat{\Omega}_{a,mm}\bm{C}^{T}})^{-1}\Psi_{n}^{1/2}\right)=O_{p}(1)
, as desired.

It remains to show (
52
).
By definition and symmetry of
{\bm{K}}_{n}
and
\tilde{{\bm{K}}}_{n,a}
, it suffices to show that
\|{\bm{K}}_{n}-\tilde{{\bm{K}}}_{n,a}\|_{\infty}=O_{p}((s+m+K)/\sqrt{n})
.

\displaystyle\|{\bm{K}}_{n}-\tilde{{\bm{K}}}_{n,a}\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle=\max_{j\in{S\cup\mathcal{M}\cup\mathcal{K}}}\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\frac{1}{n}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\left({\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}^{*})-{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a})\right)\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\right\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{j\in{S\cup\mathcal{M}\cup\mathcal{K}}}\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\frac{1}{n}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\left({\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}^{*})-{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a})\right)\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\right\|_{2}\sqrt{s+m+K}

By Taylor’s expansion, for
j\in S\cup\mathcal{M}
,

\displaystyle~\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\bm{x}_{(j)}^{T}\left({\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}^{*})-{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a})\right)\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\text{diag}\left\{\bm{x}_{(j)}\circ{\Sigma}^{\prime}(\bm{X}^{k}\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{j})\right\}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}(\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0}-{\mathcal{B}^{*}}),

for some
\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{j}
on the line segment joining
\mathcal{B}^{*}
and
\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a}
.
Since
\sup_{t}|{\Sigma}^{\prime}(t)|=O(1)
and by the 5th condition of (A1), we have

\displaystyle~\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\bm{x}_{(j)}^{T}\left({\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}^{*})-{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a})\right)\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\text{diag}\left\{|\bm{x}_{(j)}|\circ|{\Sigma}^{\prime}(\bm{X}^{k}\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{j})|\right\}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\right\|_{2}\left\|\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a}-{\mathcal{B}^{*}}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\sup_{t}|{\Sigma}^{\prime}(t)|\cdot\lambda_{\max}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\text{diag}\left\{|\bm{x}_{(j)}|\right\}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\right)\left\|\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a}-{\mathcal{B}^{*}}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~O(n)\left\|\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a}-{\mathcal{B}^{*}}\right\|_{2}.

This together with
\left\|\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a}-{\mathcal{B}^{*}}\right\|_{2}=O_{p}(\sqrt{(s+m+K)/n})
from Theorem (
1
) implies that for
j\in S\cup\mathcal{M}
,

\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\frac{1}{n}\bm{x}_{(j)}^{T}\left({\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}^{*})-{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a})\right)\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\right\|_{2}=O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{s+m+K}{{n}}}\right).

Similarly, we can prove along with the 4th condition of (A1) that for
j\in\mathcal{K}
,

\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\frac{1}{n}(\bm{x}_{(j)}^{k})^{T}\left({\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}^{*})-{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a})\right)\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\right\|_{2}=O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{s+m+K}{{n}}}\right).

Combining those together, we have

\displaystyle\|{\bm{K}}_{n}-\tilde{{\bm{K}}}_{n,a}\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{j\in{S\cup\mathcal{M}\cup\mathcal{K}}}\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\frac{1}{n}(\bm{x}_{(j)}^{k})^{T}\left({\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}^{*})-{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a})\right)\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\right\|_{2}\sqrt{m+s+K}

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{s+m+K}{{n}}}\right)\sqrt{s+m+K}=O_{p}\left(\frac{s+m+K}{\sqrt{n}}\right).

This ends the prove of equation (
39
).
∎

Proof of equation (
40
)

Proof.

By Cauchy-Schwartz inequality

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{I}-\Omega_{n}^{-1/2}\widehat{\Omega}_{0}\Omega_{n}^{-1/2}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\Omega_{n}^{-1/2}(\Omega_{n}-\widehat{\Omega}_{0})\Omega_{n}^{-1/2}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\Omega_{n}^{-1}\right\|_{2}\left\|\Omega_{n}-\widehat{\Omega}_{0}\right\|_{2}.

By the 4th condition in (A1), we have
\lambda_{\max}(\bm{K}_{n})=O(1)
. It remains to bound
\left\|\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}-\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,0}^{-1}\right\|_{2}
.
Assume for now, we have

\left\|\bm{K}_{n}-\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,0}\right\|_{2}=O_{p}\left(\frac{s+m+K}{\sqrt{n}}\right),

(57)

this together with
\lambda_{\max}(\bm{K}_{n}^{-1})=O(1)
and
s+m+K=o(\sqrt{n})
implies that with probability tending to
1
,

\displaystyle\lim\inf_{n}\lambda_{\min}(\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,0})

\displaystyle\geq\lim\inf_{n}\lambda_{\min}(\bm{K}_{n})-\lim\sup_{n}\|\bm{K}_{n}-\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,0}\|_{2}>0,

which is equivalent to
\lambda_{\max}(\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,0}^{-1})=O_{p}(1)
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}-\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,0}^{-1}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}(\bm{K}_{n}-\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,0})\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,0}^{-1}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}\right\|_{2}\left\|\bm{K}_{n}-\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,0}\right\|_{2}\left\|\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,0}^{-1}\right\|_{2}=O_{p}\left(\frac{s+m+K}{\sqrt{n}}\right).

It remains to prove equation (
57
). Similar to the prove of equation (
52
), we can show that under event
\{S=\hat{S}_{0}\}
,
\|\bm{K}_{n}-\tilde{\bm{K}}_{n,0}\|_{2}=O_{p}\left(\frac{s+m+K}{\sqrt{n}}\right)
, where

\tilde{{\bm{K}}}_{n,0}=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
\left({\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}}}\right)^{T}\end{pmatrix}{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}{\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0}})\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
\left({\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}}}\right)^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}.

This together with the fact that
\mathbb{P}(S=\hat{S}_{0})\rightarrow 1
proves the equation (
57
). This ends the proof of equation (
40
).

∎

Proof of equation (
41
), (
42
),
(
43
)

Proof.

We first prove that
\lambda_{\max}(\bm{V}_{n})=O(1)
by showing that
\lambda_{\max}(\bm{V}_{n})\leq\lambda_{\max}(\bm{K}_{n})
:

\displaystyle\lambda_{\max}(\bm{V}_{n})

\displaystyle=\sup_{\bm{a}:\|\bm{a}\|_{2}=1}\bm{a}^{T}\bm{V}_{n}\bm{a}

\displaystyle=\sup_{\bm{a}:\|\bm{a}\|_{2}=1}\frac{1}{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\bm{a}^{T}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
\left({\bm{X}^{k}_{{{S}}\cup\mathcal{K}}}\right)^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}}_{0}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\right\}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\bm{a}:\|\bm{a}\|_{2}=1}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\bm{a}^{T}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
\left({\bm{X}^{k}_{{{S}}\cup\mathcal{K}}}\right)^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}}_{0}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\right\}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\sup_{\bm{a}:\|\bm{a}\|_{2}=1}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\bm{a}^{T}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}{\mathcal{B}^{*}})\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\bm{a}

\displaystyle=\sup_{\bm{a}:\|\bm{a}\|_{2}=1}\bm{a}^{T}\bm{K}_{n}\bm{a}=\lambda_{\max}(\bm{K}_{n}),

where the inequality is from the convexity of function
\|\cdot\|_{2}^{2}
. Since
\lambda_{\max}(\bm{K}_{n})=O(1)
by the 4th condition in (A1), we have
\lambda_{\max}(\bm{V}_{n})=O(1)
. And by the 7th condition in (A1), we have
\lambda_{\max}(\bm{V}_{n}^{-1})=O(1)
. And similar to the proof equation (
52
) and equation (
57
), we can show that

\left\|\bm{V}_{n}-\widehat{\bm{V}}_{n,a}\right\|_{2}=O_{p}\left(\frac{s+m+K}{\sqrt{n}}\right),

(58)

We next prove equation (
41
) by splitting
\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}^{-1}\widehat{\bm{V}}_{n,a}\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}^{-1}-{\bm{K}}_{n}^{-1}{\bm{V}}_{n}{\bm{K}}_{n}^{-1}

into several parts:

\displaystyle~\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}^{-1}\widehat{\bm{V}}_{n,a}\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}^{-1}-{\bm{K}}_{n}^{-1}{\bm{V}}_{n}{\bm{K}}_{n}^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\underbrace{\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}^{-1}(\widehat{\bm{V}}_{n,a}-{\bm{V}}_{n})\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}^{-1}}_{I}+\underbrace{(\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}^{-1}-{\bm{K}}_{n}^{-1}){\bm{V}}_{n}(\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}^{-1}-{\bm{K}}_{n}^{-1})}_{II}

\displaystyle~+\underbrace{(\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}^{-1}-{\bm{K}}_{n}^{-1}){\bm{V}}_{n}{\bm{K}}_{n}^{-1}+{\bm{K}}_{n}^{-1}{\bm{V}}_{n}(\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}^{-1}-{\bm{K}}_{n}^{-1})}_{III}

For the term
\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}^{-1}
in
I
, we proved in equation (
53
) that
\lambda_{\max}(\tilde{\bm{K}}_{n,a}^{-1})=O_{p}(1)
. This together with the fact that
\mathbb{P}(S=\hat{S}_{a})\rightarrow 1
proves that
\lambda_{\max}(\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}^{-1})=O_{p}(1)
. Then along with equation (
58
), we have

\|{\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}^{-1}(\widehat{\bm{V}}_{n,a}-{\bm{V}}_{n})\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}^{-1}}\|_{2}\leq\|\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}^{-1}\|_{2}^{2}\cdot\|{\widehat{\bm{V}}_{n,a}-{\bm{V}}_{n}}\|_{2}=O_{p}\left(\frac{s+m+K}{\sqrt{n}}\right).

For term
(\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}^{-1}-{\bm{K}}_{n}^{-1})
in
II
, we proved in equation (
54
) that
\|{\bm{K}}_{n}^{-1}-\tilde{{\bm{K}}}_{n,a}^{-1}\|_{2}=O_{p}\left(\frac{s+m+K}{\sqrt{n}}\right)
. This together with the fact that
\mathbb{P}(S=\hat{S}_{a})\rightarrow 1
proves that
\|{\bm{K}}_{n}^{-1}-\widehat{{\bm{K}}}_{n,a}^{-1}\|_{2}=O_{p}\left(\frac{s+m+K}{\sqrt{n}}\right)
. Then along with
\lambda_{\max}(\bm{V}_{n})=O(1)
and
s+m+K=o(\sqrt{n})
, we have

\|{{(\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}^{-1}-{\bm{K}}_{n}^{-1}){\bm{V}}_{n}(\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}^{-1}-{\bm{K}}_{n}^{-1})}}\|_{2}\leq\|{\bm{V}}_{n}\|_{2}\cdot\|{\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}^{-1}-{\bm{K}}_{n}^{-1}}\|_{2}^{2}=o_{p}\left(\frac{s+m+K}{\sqrt{n}}\right).

For bounding
III
, we proved that
\lambda_{\max}(\bm{K}_{n}^{-1})=O(1)
. Then we have

\|{(\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}^{-1}-{\bm{K}}_{n}^{-1}){\bm{V}}_{n}{\bm{K}}_{n}^{-1}+{\bm{K}}_{n}^{-1}{\bm{V}}_{n}(\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}^{-1}-{\bm{K}}_{n}^{-1})}\|_{2}\leq 2\|{\bm{K}}_{n}^{-1}\|_{2}\|{\bm{V}}_{n}\|_{2}\cdot\|{\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}^{-1}-{\bm{K}}_{n}^{-1}}\|_{2}=O_{p}\left(\frac{s+m+K}{\sqrt{n}}\right).

Combining the above three results, we proved the desired equation (
41
). This, together with the fact that
s+m+K=o(\sqrt{n})
as well as
\left\|{\bm{K}}_{n}^{-1}{\bm{V}}_{n}{\bm{K}}_{n}^{-1}\right\|_{2}=O(1)
proves the result (
42
).
We can further prove result (
43
)
by proving that
\lim\inf_{n}\lambda_{\min}\left(\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}^{-1}\widehat{\bm{V}}_{n,a}\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}^{-1}\right)>0
with probability tending to
1
. Notice that

\displaystyle~\lim\inf_{n}\lambda_{\min}\left(\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}^{-1}\widehat{\bm{V}}_{n,a}\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}^{-1}\right)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle~\lim\inf_{n}\lambda_{\min}\left({\bm{K}}_{n}^{-1}{\bm{V}}_{n}{\bm{K}}_{n}^{-1}\right)-\lim\sup_{n}\left\|\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}^{-1}\widehat{\bm{V}}_{n,a}\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}^{-1}-{\bm{K}}_{n}^{-1}{\bm{V}}_{n}{\bm{K}}_{n}^{-1}\right\|_{2}.

This together with the fact that
\lambda_{\max}({\bm{K}}_{n})=O(1)
,
\lambda_{\max}({\bm{V}}_{n}^{-1})=O(1)
,
s+m+K=o(\sqrt{n})
, as well as the equation (
41
) proved above, we have
\lim\inf_{n}\lambda_{\min}\left(\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}^{-1}\widehat{\bm{V}}_{n,a}\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}^{-1}\right)>0
with probability tending to
1
.

∎

Proof of equation (
44
)

Proof.

We first prove that

\left\|\bm{I}-\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}\right\|_{2}=O_{p}\left(\frac{s+m+K}{\sqrt{n}}\right).

(59)

Notice that

\displaystyle~\bm{I}-\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}(\mathcal{T}_{n}-\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a})\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}({\bm{K}}_{n}^{-1}{\bm{V}}_{n}{\bm{K}}_{n}^{-1}-\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}^{-1}\widehat{\bm{V}}_{n,a}\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}^{-1})\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}{\bm{K}}_{n}^{-1}{\bm{V}}_{n}^{1/2}\cdot{\bm{V}}_{n}^{-1/2}{\bm{K}}_{n}({\bm{K}}_{n}^{-1}{\bm{V}}_{n}{\bm{K}}_{n}^{-1}-\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}^{-1}\widehat{\bm{V}}_{n,a}\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}^{-1}){\bm{K}}_{n}{\bm{V}}_{n}^{-1/2}\cdot{\bm{V}}_{n}^{1/2}{\bm{K}}_{n}^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}.

This implies that

\displaystyle~\left\|\bm{I}-\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\left\|{\bm{K}}_{n}^{-1}{\bm{V}}_{n}{\bm{K}}_{n}^{-1}-\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}^{-1}\widehat{\bm{V}}_{n,a}\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}^{-1}\right\|_{2}\cdot\left\|{\bm{K}}_{n}{\bm{V}}_{n}^{-1/2}\right\|_{2}^{2}\cdot\left\|{\bm{V}}_{n}^{1/2}{\bm{K}}_{n}^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\left\|{\bm{K}}_{n}^{-1}{\bm{V}}_{n}{\bm{K}}_{n}^{-1}-\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}^{-1}\widehat{\bm{V}}_{n,a}\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}^{-1}\right\|_{2}\cdot\left\|{\bm{K}}_{n}{\bm{V}}_{n}^{-1}{\bm{K}}_{n}\right\|_{2}\cdot\left\|\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}\mathcal{T}_{n}\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~O_{p}\left(\frac{s+m+K}{\sqrt{n}}\right),

where the last equality is from the definition of
\mathcal{T}_{n}
, the proved equation (
41
) and the fact that
\lambda_{\max}(\bm{K}_{n})=O(1)
,
\lambda_{\max}(\bm{V}_{n}^{-1})=O(1)
. This ends the proof of equation (
59
). We then prove that

\lambda_{\max}\left(\mathcal{T}_{n}^{1/2}\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}^{-1}\mathcal{T}_{n}^{1/2}\right)=O_{p}(1)

(60)

by proving that
\lim\inf_{n}\lambda_{\min}\left(\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}\right)>0
with probability tending to
1
. Notice that

\displaystyle~\lim\inf_{n}\lambda_{\min}\left(\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}\right)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle~\lim\inf_{n}\lambda_{\min}\left(\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}{\mathcal{T}}_{n}\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}\right)-\lim\sup_{n}\left\|\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}({\mathcal{T}}_{n}-\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a})\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~1-\lim\sup_{n}\left\|\bm{I}-\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}\right\|_{2}

This together with the fact that
s+m+K=o(\sqrt{n})
, as well as the equation (
59
) proved above, implies that
\lim\inf_{n}\lambda_{\min}\left(\mathcal{T}_{n}^{1/2}\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}^{-1}\mathcal{T}_{n}^{1/2}\right)>0
with probability tending to
1
. Finally, combining the results (
59
) and (
60
), we have

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{I}-\mathcal{T}_{n}^{1/2}\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}^{-1}\mathcal{T}_{n}^{1/2}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\left(\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}-\bm{I}\right)\mathcal{T}_{n}^{1/2}\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}^{-1}\mathcal{T}_{n}^{1/2}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}-\bm{I}\right\|_{2}\left\|\mathcal{T}_{n}^{1/2}\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}^{-1}\mathcal{T}_{n}^{1/2}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{s+m+K}{\sqrt{n}}\right).

This ends the proof of equation (
44
).
∎

Proof of equation (
45
)

Proof.

\displaystyle~\left\|\Psi_{n}\left(\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}^{-1}-\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1}\right)\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\left\|\Psi_{n}\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}\left(\mathcal{T}_{n}^{1/2}\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}^{-1}\mathcal{T}_{n}^{1/2}-\bm{I}\right)\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\left\|\Psi_{n}\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}\right\|_{2}\left\|\mathcal{T}_{n}^{1/2}\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}^{-1}\mathcal{T}_{n}^{1/2}-\bm{I}\right\|_{2}\left\|\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}\right\|_{2}.

Notice that

\displaystyle~\left\|\Psi_{n}\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\left\|\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\left\|\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}\bm{V}_{n}^{1/2}\cdot\bm{V}_{n}^{-1/2}\bm{K}_{n}\bm{V}_{n}^{-1/2}\cdot\bm{V}_{n}^{1/2}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\left\|\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}\bm{V}_{n}^{1/2}\right\|_{2}\cdot\left\|\bm{V}_{n}^{-1/2}\bm{K}_{n}\bm{V}_{n}^{-1/2}\right\|_{2}\cdot\left\|\bm{V}_{n}^{1/2}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\left\|\mathcal{T}_{n}^{1/2}\right\|_{2}\cdot\left\|\bm{V}_{n}^{-1/2}\bm{K}_{n}\bm{V}_{n}^{-1/2}\right\|_{2}\cdot\left\|\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}\mathcal{T}_{n}\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}\right\|_{2}.

Then we have

\displaystyle~\left\|\Psi_{n}\left(\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}^{-1}-\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1}\right)\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\left\|\mathcal{T}_{n}^{1/2}\right\|_{2}\cdot\left\|\bm{V}_{n}^{-1/2}\bm{K}_{n}\bm{V}_{n}^{-1/2}\right\|_{2}\cdot\left\|\mathcal{T}_{n}^{1/2}\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}^{-1}\mathcal{T}_{n}^{1/2}-\bm{I}\right\|_{2}\cdot\left\|\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\left\|\bm{V}_{n}^{-1/2}\bm{K}_{n}\bm{V}_{n}^{-1/2}\right\|_{2}\cdot\left\|\mathcal{T}_{n}^{1/2}\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}^{-1}\mathcal{T}_{n}^{1/2}-\bm{I}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~O_{p}\left(\frac{s+m+K}{\sqrt{n}}\right),

where the last equation is from the fact that
\lambda_{\max}(\bm{K}_{n})=O(1)
,
\lambda_{\max}(\bm{V}_{n}^{-1})=O(1)
as well as equation (
44
). This ends the proof of equation (
45
).
∎

Proof of equation (
46
), (
47
),
(
48
)

Proof.

Notice that

\displaystyle~{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}{\widehat{\Psi}_{n,a}}^{-1}{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}-{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{1/2}{\Psi}_{n}^{-1}{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{1/2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}\left({\widehat{\Psi}_{n,a}}^{-1}-{\Psi}_{n}^{-1}\right){\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}+\left({\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}{\Psi}_{n}^{-1}{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}-{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{1/2}{\Psi}_{n}^{-1}{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{1/2}\right).

(61)

For the first term, we have

\displaystyle~\left\|{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}\left({\widehat{\Psi}_{n,a}}^{-1}-{\Psi}_{n}^{-1}\right){\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\left\|{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}{\Psi}_{n}^{-1/2}\left({\Psi}_{n}^{1/2}{\widehat{\Psi}_{n,a}}^{-1}{\Psi}_{n}^{1/2}-\bm{I}\right){\Psi}_{n}^{-1/2}{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\left\|{\Psi}_{n}^{1/2}{\widehat{\Psi}_{n,a}}^{-1}{\Psi}_{n}^{1/2}-\bm{I}\right\|_{2}\left\|{\Psi}_{n}^{-1/2}{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}\right\|_{2}^{2}.

By result (
39
) , we have
\left\|{\Psi}_{n}^{1/2}{\widehat{\Psi}_{n,a}}^{-1}{\Psi}_{n}^{1/2}-\bm{I}\right\|_{2}=O_{p}\left(\frac{s+m+K}{\sqrt{n}}\right)
. For
\left\|{\Psi}_{n}^{-1/2}{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}\right\|_{2}^{2}=\left\|{\Psi}_{n}^{-1/2}{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}{\Psi}_{n}^{-1/2}\right\|_{2}
,
notice that

\displaystyle~\left\|{\Psi}_{n}^{-1/2}{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}{\Psi}_{n}^{-1/2}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\left\|{\Psi}_{n}^{-1/2}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}^{-1}\widehat{\bm{V}}_{n,a}\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}{\Psi}_{n}^{-1/2}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\left\|{\Psi}_{n}^{-1/2}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}\cdot\bm{K}_{n}^{1/2}\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}^{-1}\widehat{\bm{V}}_{n,a}\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}^{-1}\bm{K}_{n}^{1/2}\cdot\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}{\Psi}_{n}^{-1/2}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\left\|\bm{K}_{n}^{1/2}\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}^{-1}\widehat{\bm{V}}_{n,a}\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}^{-1}\bm{K}_{n}^{1/2}\right\|_{2}\cdot\left\|\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}{\Psi}_{n}^{-1/2}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\left\|\bm{K}_{n}\right\|_{2}\left\|\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}^{-1}\widehat{\bm{V}}_{n,a}\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}^{-1}\right\|_{2}\cdot\left\|{\Psi}_{n}^{-1/2}{\Psi}_{n}{\Psi}_{n}^{-1/2}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~O_{p}\left(\frac{s+m+K}{\sqrt{n}}\right),

where the last equation is from the definition of
\Psi_{n}
as well as the fact that
\lambda_{\max}(\bm{K}_{n})=O(1)
and result (
42
) proved before. Therefore, we proved that
\left\|{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}\left({\widehat{\Psi}_{n,a}}^{-1}-{\Psi}_{n}^{-1}\right){\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}\right\|_{2}=O_{p}\left(\frac{s+m+K}{\sqrt{n}}\right)
.

For the second term in equation (
61
), assume for now that we have the following result

\left\|{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{-1/2}{\Psi}_{n}{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{-1/2}-{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{-1/2}{\Psi}_{n}{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{-1/2}\right\|_{2}=O_{p}\left(\frac{s+m+K}{\sqrt{n}}\right).

(62)

Next, we prove that
\left\|{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{1/2}{\Psi}_{n}^{-1}{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{1/2}\right\|_{2}=O(1)
and
\left\|{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}{\Psi}_{n}^{-1}{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}\right\|_{2}=O_{p}(1)
. Notice that

\displaystyle~\lim\inf_{n}\lambda_{\min}\left({{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{-1/2}{\Psi}_{n}{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{-1/2}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\lim\inf_{n}\min_{\bm{a}:\|\bm{a}\|_{2}=1}\bm{a}^{T}{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{-1/2}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}{\bm{K}}_{n}^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{-1/2}\bm{a}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\lim\inf_{n}\min_{\bm{a}:\|\bm{a}\|_{2}=1}\bm{a}^{T}{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{-1/2}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}{\bm{K}}_{n}^{-1}\bm{V}_{n}^{1/2}\cdot\bm{V}_{n}^{-1/2}{\bm{K}}_{n}\bm{V}_{n}^{-1/2}\cdot\bm{V}_{n}^{1/2}{\bm{K}}_{n}^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{-1/2}\bm{a}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle~\lim\inf_{n}\lambda_{\min}\left(\bm{V}_{n}^{-1/2}{\bm{K}}_{n}\bm{V}_{n}^{-1/2}\right)\lambda_{\min}\left({{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{-1/2}{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{-1/2}\right)>0,

(63)

where the last inequality is from the definition of
\Psi_{n}
as well as the fact that
\lambda_{\max}(\bm{K}_{n}^{-1})=O(1)
and
\lambda_{\max}(\bm{V}_{n})=O(1)
. Similarly, we can prove result (
48
). By the definition of
\Psi_{n}
and
{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}
, we have

\displaystyle~\lim\inf_{n}\lambda_{\min}\left({\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{-1/2}{\Psi}_{n}{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{-1/2}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\lim\inf_{n}\min_{\bm{a}:\|\bm{a}\|_{2}=1}\bm{a}^{T}{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{-1/2}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}^{-1}\widehat{\bm{V}}_{n,a}^{1/2}\cdot\widehat{\bm{V}}_{n,a}^{-1/2}\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}{\bm{K}}_{n}^{-1}\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}\widehat{\bm{V}}_{n,a}^{-1/2}\cdot\widehat{\bm{V}}_{n,a}^{1/2}\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{-1/2}\bm{a}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle~\lim\inf_{n}\lambda_{\min}\left(\widehat{\bm{V}}_{n,a}^{-1/2}\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}{\bm{K}}_{n}^{-1}\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}\widehat{\bm{V}}_{n,a}^{-1/2}\right)>0,

(64)

where the last inequality is because

\displaystyle\left\|\widehat{\bm{V}}_{n,a}^{1/2}\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}^{-1}{\bm{K}}_{n}\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}^{-1}\widehat{\bm{V}}_{n,a}^{1/2}\right\|_{2}\leq\left\|{\bm{K}}_{n}\right\|_{2}\left\|\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}^{-1}\widehat{\bm{V}}_{n,a}\widehat{\bm{K}}_{n,a}^{-1}\right\|_{2}=O_{p}(1)

along with the fact that
\lambda_{\max}(\bm{K}_{n}^{-1})=O(1)
and result (
42
) proved before.
Then, we have

\displaystyle~\left\|{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}{\Psi}_{n}^{-1}{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}-{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{1/2}{\Psi}_{n}^{-1}{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{1/2}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\left\|{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}{\Psi}_{n}^{-1}{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}({\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{-1/2}{\Psi}_{n}{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{-1/2}-{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{-1/2}{\Psi}_{n}{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{-1/2}){\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}{\Psi}_{n}^{-1}{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\left\|{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}{\Psi}_{n}^{-1}{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}\right\|_{2}\left\|{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{-1/2}{\Psi}_{n}{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{-1/2}-{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{-1/2}{\Psi}_{n}{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{-1/2}\right\|_{2}\left\|{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}{\Psi}_{n}^{-1}{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~O_{p}(1)\cdot O_{p}\left(\frac{s+m+K}{\sqrt{n}}\right)\cdot O(1)=O_{p}\left(\frac{s+m+K}{\sqrt{n}}\right).

So it remains to prove equation (
62
). We start from proving that

\left\|{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{-1}{\Psi}_{n}^{2}{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{-1}-{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{-1}{\Psi}_{n}^{2}{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{-1}\right\|_{2}=O_{p}\left(\frac{s+m+K}{\sqrt{n}}\right).

(65)

Notice that

\displaystyle~\left\|{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{-1}{\Psi}_{n}^{2}{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{-1}-{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{-1}{\Psi}_{n}^{2}{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{-1}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\left\|({\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{-1}-{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{-1}){\Psi}_{n}^{2}({\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{-1}-{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{-1})+({\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{-1}-{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{-1}){\Psi}_{n}^{2}{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{-1}+{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{-1}{\Psi}_{n}^{2}({\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{-1}-{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{-1})\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\left\|{\Psi}_{n}({\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{-1}-{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{-1})\right\|_{2}^{2}+2\left\|{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{-1}{\Psi}_{n}\right\|_{2}\left\|{\Psi}_{n}({\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{-1}-{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{-1})\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~O_{p}\left(\frac{(s+m+K)^{2}}{n}\right)+\left\|{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{-1}{\Psi}_{n}\right\|_{2}O_{p}\left(\frac{s+m+K}{\sqrt{n}}\right),

where the last equation is from the result (
45
) proved before.
And
\left\|{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{-1}{\Psi}_{n}\right\|_{2}=O(1)
because

\displaystyle~\left\|{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{-1}{\Psi}_{n}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\left\|{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{-1/2}\right\|_{2}\left\|{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{-1/2}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}{\bm{K}}_{n}^{-1}{\bm{V}}_{n}^{1/2}\cdot{\bm{V}}_{n}^{-1/2}{\bm{K}}_{n}{\bm{V}}_{n}^{-1/2}\cdot{\bm{V}}_{n}^{1/2}{\bm{K}}_{n}^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\left\|{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{-1/2}\right\|_{2}\left\|{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{-1/2}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}{\bm{K}}_{n}^{-1}{\bm{V}}_{n}^{1/2}\right\|_{2}\left\|{\bm{V}}_{n}^{-1/2}{\bm{K}}_{n}{\bm{V}}_{n}^{-1/2}\right\|_{2}\left\|{\bm{V}}_{n}^{1/2}{\bm{K}}_{n}^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\left\|{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{-1/2}\right\|_{2}\cdot 1\cdot O(1)\cdot\left\|{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{1/2}\right\|_{2}=O(1).

Along with the fact that
s+m+K=o(\sqrt{n})
, we proved the desired equation (
65
). By applying Lemma
1
, we have

\displaystyle~\left\|{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{-1/2}{\Psi}_{n}{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{-1/2}-{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{-1/2}{\Psi}_{n}{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{-1/2}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\left\|{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{-1}{\Psi}_{n}^{2}{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{-1}-{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{-1}{\Psi}_{n}^{2}{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{-1}\right\|_{2}\frac{1}{\lambda_{\min}({\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{-1/2}{\Psi}_{n}{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{-1/2}+{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{-1/2}{\Psi}_{n}{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{-1/2})}.

From results (
63
) and (
64
), we have
1/{\lambda_{\min}({\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{-1/2}{\Psi}_{n}{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{-1/2}+{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{-1/2}{\Psi}_{n}{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{-1/2})}=O_{p}(1)
.
Along with result (
65
), we proved the equation (
62
) as desired. This ends the proof for equation (
46
).

∎

Proof of equation (
49
)

Proof.

First proof that
\lambda_{\max}(\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1})=O(1)
. By definition of
\mathcal{T}
, we have

\displaystyle\lim\inf_{n}\lambda_{\min}(\mathcal{T}_{n})

\displaystyle=\lim\inf_{n}\min_{\bm{a}:\|\bm{a}\|_{2}=1}\bm{a}^{T}\mathcal{T}_{n}\bm{a}

\displaystyle=\lim\inf_{n}\min_{\bm{a}:\|\bm{a}\|_{2}=1}\bm{a}^{T}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}{\bm{K}}_{n}^{-1}{\bm{V}}_{n}{\bm{K}}_{n}^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{a}

\displaystyle\geq\lim\inf_{n}\lambda_{\min}\left({\bm{K}}_{n}^{-1}{\bm{V}}_{n}{\bm{K}}_{n}^{-1}\right)\cdot\lim\inf_{n}\lambda_{\min}\left(\bm{C}\bm{C}^{T}\right)>0,

where the last inequality is from the fact that
\lambda_{\max}(\bm{K}_{n})=O(1)
,
\lambda_{\max}(\bm{V}_{n}^{-1})=O(1)
, as well as
\lambda_{\max}((\bm{C}\bm{C}^{T})^{-1})=O(1)
given in assumption (A3). Along with the fact that
\|\bm{h}_{n}\|_{2}=O(\sqrt{\min(m+s+K-r,r)/n})\leq O(\sqrt{r/n})
given in assumption (A3), we proved the desired result (
49
).
∎

A.2
Proof of Theorem
1

Proof.

Since the true coefficients satisfy
\mathcal{B}^{*}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K})^{c}}=\bm{0}
, we define an oracle estimator
\hat{\mathcal{B}}
as solution to

\max_{\mathcal{B}}Q_{n}(\mathcal{B})~~\text{s.t.}~\bm{C}\mathcal{B}_{\mathcal{M}}=\bm{t},~\mathcal{B}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K})^{c}}=\bm{0}.

(66)

Step 1: Show that
\|\hat{\mathcal{B}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}\|_{2}=O_{p}(\sqrt{(m+s+K-r)/n})
.

Define a
(p+K)
-dimensional vector
\mathcal{B}_{0}
as

\begin{cases}\mathcal{B}_{0,\mathcal{M}}&=\mathcal{B}_{\mathcal{M}}^{*}-\bm{C}^{T}(\bm{C}\bm{C}^{T})^{-1}\bm{h}_{n},\\
\mathcal{B}_{0,\mathcal{M}^{c}}&=\mathcal{B}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{*}.\end{cases}

It is immediate to see that

\bm{C}\mathcal{B}_{0,\mathcal{M}}-\bm{t}=\bm{0}.

And it follows from (A3) that

\|\mathcal{B}_{0}-\mathcal{B}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}=\|\bm{C}^{T}(\bm{C}\bm{C}^{T})^{-1}\bm{h}_{n}\|_{2}^{2}\leq\lambda_{\max}((\bm{C}\bm{C}^{T})^{-1})\|\bm{h}_{n}\|_{2}^{2}\leq O((m+s+K-r)/n).

(67)

So it suffices to show that
\|\hat{\mathcal{B}}-\mathcal{B}_{0}\|_{2}^{2}=O_{p}((m+s+K-r)/n)
.

Observe that
\bm{C}\mathcal{B}_{0,\mathcal{M}}=\bm{t}
, then for any
\mathcal{B}
with
\bm{C}\mathcal{B}_{\mathcal{M}}=\bm{t}
, we have
\bm{C}(\mathcal{B}_{\mathcal{M}}-\mathcal{B}_{0,\mathcal{M}})=\bm{0}
, i.e.,
\mathcal{B}_{\mathcal{M}}-\mathcal{B}_{0,\mathcal{M}}\in Null(\bm{C})
. Take basis matrix
\bm{Z}\in\mathbb{R}^{m\times(m-r)}
of
Null(\bm{C})
, i.e.,
\bm{C}\bm{Z}=\bm{0}
, and
\bm{Z}^{T}\bm{Z}=\bm{I}_{m-r}
. Then for any
\mathcal{B}_{\mathcal{M}}
s.t.
\bm{C}\mathcal{B}_{\mathcal{M}}=\bm{t}
,
\exists\bm{v}\in\mathbb{R}^{m-r}
, s.t.
\mathcal{B}_{\mathcal{M}}=\mathcal{B}_{0,\mathcal{M}}+\bm{Z}\bm{v}
. Then define
\overline{Q}_{n}(\bm{\delta})=Q_{n}(\mathcal{B}(\bm{\delta}))
for any
\bm{\delta}\in\mathbb{R}^{(m-r)+s+K}
where
\mathcal{B}(\bm{\delta})
is defined as

\begin{cases}\mathcal{B}(\bm{\delta})_{\mathcal{M}}&=\mathcal{B}_{0,\mathcal{M}}+\bm{Z}\bm{\delta}_{J_{0}},\\
\mathcal{B}(\bm{\delta})_{S\cup\mathcal{K}}&=\mathcal{B}_{0,S\cup\mathcal{K}}+\bm{\delta}_{J_{0}^{c}},\\
\mathcal{B}(\bm{\delta})_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K})^{c}}&=\mathcal{B}_{0,(\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K})^{c}},\end{cases}

where
J_{0}=[1,2,\dots,m-r]
. Then solving the constrained optimization problem (
66
) is equivalent to solving
\max_{\bm{\delta}\in\mathbb{R}^{(m-r)+s+K}}\overline{Q}_{n}(\bm{\delta})
.
Since
\|\bm{Z}\bm{\delta}_{J_{0}}\|_{2}^{2}=\|\bm{\delta}_{J_{0}}\|_{2}^{2}
, it suffices to show that there exists a local maximizer
\bm{\delta}_{0}
of
\overline{Q}_{n}(\bm{\delta})
such that
\|\bm{\delta}_{0}\|_{2}=O_{p}(\sqrt{(m+s+K-r)/n})
.

Define a neighborhood
\mathcal{N}_{\tau}=\{\bm{\delta}\in\mathbb{R}^{(m-r)+s+K}:\|\bm{\delta}\|_{2}\leq\tau\sqrt{(m+s+K-r)/n}\}
, and an event
H_{n}:=\left\{\overline{Q}_{n}(\bm{0})>\max_{\bm{\delta}\in{\partial\mathcal{N}_{\tau}}}\overline{Q}_{n}(\bm{\delta})\right\}
, where
{\partial\mathcal{N}_{\tau}}
denotes the boundary of
{\mathcal{N}_{\tau}}
. On the event
H_{n}
, there must exist a local maximizer in
\mathcal{N}_{\tau}
. Hence, it suffices to show
\mathbb{P}(H_{n})\rightarrow 1
as
n\rightarrow\infty
and some sufficiently large
\tau
.

For any
\bm{\delta}\in\partial\mathcal{N}_{\tau}
, by the second-order Taylor’s expansion

\overline{Q}_{n}(\bm{\delta})-\overline{Q}_{n}(\bm{0})=\bm{\delta}^{T}\bm{\nu}-\frac{1}{2}\bm{\delta}^{T}\bm{D}\bm{\delta},

(68)

where

\displaystyle\bm{\nu}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\frac{1}{n}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{Z}^{T}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
\bm{X}_{S}^{T}\\
-\bm{E}_{k}^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}_{0})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}_{0})\right\}-\begin{pmatrix}\bm{0}_{m-r}\\
\lambda_{n,0}\bar{\rho}(\mathcal{B}_{0,S},\lambda_{n,0})\\
\bm{0}_{K}\end{pmatrix},

\displaystyle\bm{D}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\frac{1}{n}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{Z}^{T}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
\bm{X}_{S}^{T}\\
-\bm{E}_{k}^{T}\end{pmatrix}\hat{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}^{**})\begin{pmatrix}\bm{Z}^{T}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
\bm{X}_{S}^{T}\\
-\bm{E}_{k}^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}-\begin{pmatrix}\bm{0}_{(m-r)\times(m-r)}&\bm{0}_{(m-r)\times s}&\bm{0}_{(m-r)\times K}\\
\bm{0}_{s\times(m-r)}&\Lambda_{0}&\bm{0}_{s\times K}\\
\bm{0}_{K\times(m-r)}&\bm{0}_{K\times s}&\bm{0}_{K\times K}\end{pmatrix},

where
\mathcal{B}^{**}
lies in the line segment joining
\mathcal{B}(\bm{\delta})
and
\mathcal{B}_{0}
, and
\Lambda_{0}
is a
r\times r
diagonal matrix with non-negative diagonal elements and

\displaystyle\bar{\rho}(\mathcal{B}_{S},\lambda_{n,0})

\displaystyle=\left(\text{sign}(\mathcal{B}_{j})\rho^{\prime}(|\mathcal{B}_{j}|,\lambda_{n,0}),j\in S\right)^{T}

\displaystyle\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B})

\displaystyle=\text{diag}\left\{h^{\prime}((\bm{x}_{i}^{k})^{T}\mathcal{B}),~i=1,\dots,n\right\},

\displaystyle\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B})

\displaystyle=\begin{pmatrix}\Phi((\bm{x}_{1}^{k})^{T}\mathcal{B}),\cdots,\Phi((\bm{x}_{n}^{k})^{T}\mathcal{B})\end{pmatrix}^{T},

\displaystyle\hat{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B})

\displaystyle=\text{diag}\left\{\tilde{y}_{ki}\ell^{\prime\prime}((\bm{x}_{i}^{k})^{T}\mathcal{B})+(1-\tilde{y}_{ki})\ell^{\prime\prime}(-(\bm{x}_{i}^{k})^{T}\mathcal{B}),~i=1,\dots,n\right\},

where
\ell(t)=-\log(\Phi(t))
is the probit loss function introduced and studied in
Zhou and Zou 2023
with
\ell(t)^{\prime\prime}
bounded within
[0,1]
. By the definition of
\mathcal{B}(\bm{\delta})
and
\mathcal{B}_{0}
, we have
\mathcal{B}^{**}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K})^{c}}=\bm{0}
. Moreover, it follows from equation (
67
) and
\bm{\delta}\in\partial\mathcal{N}_{\tau}
that

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{B}^{**}-\mathcal{B}^{*}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\tau\sqrt{(s-r+m+K)/n}+O(\sqrt{(s-r+m+K)/n})

\displaystyle\ll\sqrt{(s+m+K)\log(n)/n},

(69)

for
n
sufficiently large and for
\tau\ll\sqrt{\log{n}}
. Therefore, we have
\mathcal{B}^{**}\in\mathcal{N}^{*}
. By definition of
\kappa_{0,0}
, we have
\lambda_{\max}(\Lambda_{0})\leq\lambda_{n,0}\kappa_{0,0}
. Let

\bm{L}=\begin{pmatrix}\bm{Z}&\bm{0}_{m\times(s+K)}\\
\bm{0}_{(s+K)\times(m-r)}&\bm{I}_{s+K}\end{pmatrix},

we have

\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\frac{1}{n}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{Z}^{T}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\hat{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}^{**})\begin{pmatrix}\bm{Z}^{T}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}=\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\frac{1}{n}\bm{L}^{T}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\hat{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}^{**})\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\bm{L}.

Then on the event defined below:

E_{n}:=\left\{\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\frac{1}{n}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\left(\hat{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}^{*})-{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}^{*})\right)\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\right\|_{2}<\frac{c}{8}\right\},

(70)

where
{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B})=\text{diag}\left\{\frac{\varphi^{2}}{\Phi(1-\Phi)}((\bm{x}_{i}^{k})^{T}\mathcal{B}),~i=1,\dots,n\right\}
, and
c
is the constant in the 6th condition of (A1),
we must have

\lambda_{\min}\left\{\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\frac{1}{n}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{Z}^{T}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\hat{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}^{**})\begin{pmatrix}\bm{Z}^{T}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\right\}\geq\frac{3}{4}c,

(71)

for sufficiently large
n
. This is because for any
\bm{\delta}\in\mathbb{R}^{m-r+s+K}
, notice that
\|\bm{L}\bm{\delta}\|_{2}=\|\bm{\delta}\|_{2}
, then

\displaystyle~\bm{\delta}^{T}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\frac{1}{n}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{Z}^{T}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\hat{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}^{**})\begin{pmatrix}\bm{Z}^{T}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\bm{\delta}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~(\bm{L}\bm{\delta})^{T}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\frac{1}{n}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\left({\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}^{**})+\hat{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}^{**})-{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}^{**})\right)\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}(\bm{L}\bm{\delta})

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle~\lambda_{\min}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\frac{1}{n}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}^{**})\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\right)\|\bm{L}\bm{\delta}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle~\lambda_{\max}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\frac{1}{n}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\left(\hat{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}^{**})-{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}^{**})\right)\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\right)\|\bm{L}\bm{\delta}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle~c\|\bm{\delta}\|_{2}^{2}-\lambda_{\max}(\bm{U_{n}})\|\bm{\delta}\|_{2}^{2},

(72)

where the last inequality is from the fact that
\mathcal{B}^{**}\in\mathcal{N}^{*}
and the 6th condition of (A1), and

\bm{U}_{n}:=\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\frac{1}{n}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\left(\hat{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}^{**})-{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}^{**})\right)\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}.

Since

\displaystyle\hat{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}^{**})-{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}^{**})

\displaystyle=\left(\hat{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}^{**})-\hat{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}^{*})\right)+\left({\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}^{*})-{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}^{**})\right)

\displaystyle+\left(\hat{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}^{*})-{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}^{*})\right),

we have
\bm{U}_{n}=\bm{U}_{n}^{1}+\bm{U}_{n}^{2}+\bm{U}_{n}^{3}
, where

\displaystyle\bm{U}_{n}^{1}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\frac{1}{n}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\left(\hat{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}^{**})-\hat{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}^{*})\right)\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T},

\displaystyle\bm{U}_{n}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\frac{1}{n}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\left({\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}^{*})-{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}^{**})\right)\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T},

\displaystyle\bm{U}_{n}^{3}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\frac{1}{n}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\left(\hat{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}^{*})-{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}^{*})\right)\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}.

By the 1st condition of (A1) and the fact that
\|\mathcal{B}^{**}-\mathcal{B}^{*}\|_{2}\ll\sqrt{(s+m+K)\log(n)/n}
as long as
\tau\ll\sqrt{\log(n)}
proved in (
69
), we have
\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\max_{1\leq k\leq K}|(\bm{x}_{i}^{k})^{T}(\mathcal{B}^{**}-\mathcal{B}^{*})|=o(1)
. This, combined with the 4th condition of (A1), implies that
\lambda_{\max}(\bm{U}_{n}^{1})<c/16
and
\lambda_{\max}(\bm{U}_{n}^{2})<c/16
when
n
is sufficiently large. Therefore, on event
E_{n}
, we must have

\lambda_{\max}(\bm{U}_{n})\leq\lambda_{\max}(\bm{U}_{n}^{1})+\lambda_{\max}(\bm{U}_{n}^{2})+\lambda_{\max}(\bm{U}_{n}^{3})\leq\frac{c}{4},

for sufficiently large
n
. This, combined with inequality (
72
), implies that

\displaystyle\bm{\delta}^{T}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\frac{1}{n}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{Z}^{T}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\hat{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}^{**})\begin{pmatrix}\bm{Z}^{T}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\bm{\delta}\geq c\|\bm{\delta}\|_{2}^{2}-\frac{c}{4}\|\bm{\delta}\|_{2}^{2}=\frac{3c}{4}\|\bm{\delta}\|_{2}^{2},

i.e., inequality (
71
) holds on event
E_{n}
.

By condition
\lambda_{n,0}\kappa_{0,0}=o(1)
in (A2), we have
\lambda_{\max}(\Lambda_{0})=o(1)
. Thus, combining with inequality (
71
), we have
\lambda_{\min}(\bm{D})\geq\frac{1}{2}{c}
for some sufficiently large
n
. Therefore, on the event
E_{n}
defined in (
70
), it follows from equation (
68
) that

\displaystyle\sup_{\bm{\delta}\in\partial\mathcal{N}_{\tau}}\overline{Q}_{n}(\bm{\delta})-\overline{Q}_{n}(\bm{0})

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\bm{\delta}\in\partial\mathcal{N}_{\tau}}\left(\|\bm{\delta}\|_{2}\|\bm{\nu}\|_{2}-\frac{1}{2}\lambda_{\min}(D)\|\bm{\delta}\|_{2}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\bm{\delta}\in\partial\mathcal{N}_{\tau}}\left(\|\bm{\delta}\|_{2}\|\bm{\nu}\|_{2}-\frac{c}{4}\|\bm{\delta}\|_{2}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\|\bm{\nu}\|_{2}\tau\sqrt{\frac{m-r+s+K}{n}}-\frac{c\tau^{2}}{4}\frac{(m-r+s+K)}{n}.

By Markov’s inequality,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}(H_{n})

\displaystyle\geq\mathbb{P}(H_{n}\cap E_{n})

\displaystyle\geq\mathbb{P}\left(\left\{\|\bm{\nu}\|_{2}<\bar{c}\tau\sqrt{\frac{m-r+s+K}{n}}\right\}\cap E_{n}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\mathbb{P}\left(\|\bm{\nu}\|_{2}<\bar{c}\tau\sqrt{\frac{m-r+s+K}{n}}\right)-\mathbb{P}(E_{n}^{c})

\displaystyle\geq 1-\frac{n\mathbb{E}\|\bm{\nu}\|_{2}^{2}}{\bar{c}^{2}\tau^{2}(m-r+s+K)}-\mathbb{P}(E_{n}^{c}),

for any fixed
\tau
satisfying
\tau\leq\frac{1}{2}\sqrt{\log n}
and for
n
sufficiently large.

Next show that
\mathbb{E}\|\bm{\nu}\|_{2}^{2}=O((m-r+s+K)/n)
. By Cauchy-Schwartz inequality,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\|\bm{\nu}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\|\lambda_{n,0}\bar{\rho}(\mathcal{B}_{0,S};\lambda_{n,0})\|_{2}^{2}+2\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\frac{1}{n}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{Z}^{T}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}_{0})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}_{0})\right\}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\Delta}{=}2I_{0}+2I_{1}

By the concavity of
\rho
, its derivative
\rho^{\prime}
is monotone decreasing. Along with the definition of
d_{n}
, and the 2nd condition of (A2), we have

I_{0}\leq s\left\{\lambda_{n,0}\rho^{\prime}(d_{n})\right\}^{2}=o(1/n).

Next show that
I_{1}=O(\frac{m-r+s+K}{n})
:

\displaystyle I_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{Z}^{T}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}_{0})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}_{0})\right\}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\left\|\begin{pmatrix}\bm{Z}^{T}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}_{0})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}_{0})\right\}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\text{tr}\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{Z}^{T}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\text{diag}\left\{(h^{\prime})^{2}((\bm{x}_{i}^{k})^{T}\mathcal{B}_{0})\cdot\Phi(1-\Phi)((\bm{x}_{i}^{k})^{T}\mathcal{B}^{*}),i=1,\dots,n\right\}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{Z}^{T}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{n}\cdot\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\left\|\begin{pmatrix}\bm{Z}^{T}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}_{0})\left\{\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}_{0})-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}^{*})\right\}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\Delta}{=}\frac{1}{n}I_{2}+\frac{1}{n}I_{3},

where the first inequality is by the convexity of
\|\cdot\|_{2}^{2}
.

To bound
I_{2}
, consider for any
i=1,\dots,n
,
k=1,\dots,K
,

\displaystyle~(h^{\prime})^{2}((\bm{x}_{i}^{k})^{T}\mathcal{B}_{0})\cdot\Phi(1-\Phi)((\bm{x}_{i}^{k})^{T}\mathcal{B}^{*})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\left[h^{\prime}((\bm{x}_{i}^{k})^{T}\mathcal{B}^{*})+h^{\prime\prime}((\bm{x}_{i}^{k})^{T}\mathcal{B}^{**})\bm{x}_{i,\mathcal{M}}^{T}(\mathcal{B}_{0,\mathcal{M}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}})\right]^{2}\cdot\Phi(1-\Phi)((\bm{x}_{i}^{k})^{T}\mathcal{B}^{*})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\frac{\varphi^{2}}{\Phi(1-\Phi)}((\bm{x}_{i}^{k})^{T}\mathcal{B}^{*})+2\varphi((\bm{x}_{i}^{k})^{T}\mathcal{B})h^{\prime\prime}((\bm{x}_{i}^{k})^{T}\mathcal{B}^{**})\bm{x}_{i,\mathcal{M}}^{T}(\mathcal{B}_{0,\mathcal{M}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}})

\displaystyle~+\left[h^{\prime\prime}((\bm{x}_{i}^{k})^{T}\mathcal{B}^{**})\bm{x}_{i,\mathcal{M}}^{T}(\mathcal{B}_{0,\mathcal{M}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}})\right]^{2}\cdot\Phi(1-\Phi)((\bm{x}_{i}^{k})^{T}\mathcal{B}^{*})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~4{\varphi(0)^{2}}+O(\bm{x}_{i,\mathcal{M}}^{T}(\mathcal{B}_{0,\mathcal{M}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}}))+O(\left[\bm{x}_{i,\mathcal{M}}^{T}(\mathcal{B}_{0,\mathcal{M}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}})\right]^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~4{\varphi(0)^{2}}+o(1)

where the first equality is from the Taylor’s expansion and
\mathcal{B}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{*}=\mathcal{B}_{0,\mathcal{M}^{c}}
; the inequality is from the fact that
\frac{\varphi^{2}}{\Phi(1-\Phi)}(t)\leq\frac{\varphi^{2}}{\Phi(1-\Phi)}(0)
,
\varphi(t)\leq\varphi(0)
,
|h^{\prime\prime}(t)|\leq 1
and
\Phi(1-\Phi)(t)\leq 1/4
; the last equality is because
\bm{x}_{i,\mathcal{M}}^{T}(\mathcal{B}_{0,\mathcal{M}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}})=o(1)
derived from the 1st condition of (A1) and the fact that
\|\mathcal{B}_{\mathcal{M}}^{*}-\mathcal{B}_{0,\mathcal{M}}\|_{2}=O(\sqrt{(m-r+s+K)/n})
. Therefore, we have

\displaystyle I_{2}\leq

\displaystyle~\text{tr}\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{Z}^{T}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{Z}^{T}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\right]\cdot O(1)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\text{tr}\left[\bm{L}^{T}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\bm{L}\right]\cdot O\left(1\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~(m-r+s+K)\cdot\lambda_{\max}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\right)\cdot O\left(1\right).

Along with the 4th condition of (A1), we have
I_{2}/n=O((m-r+s+K)/n)
.

To bound
I_{3}
, consider the
i
-th coordinate of vector
\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}_{0})\left(\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}_{0})-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}^{*})\right)
for any
i=1,\dots,n
,
k=1,\dots,K
,

\displaystyle~h^{\prime}((\bm{x}_{i}^{k})^{T}\mathcal{B}_{0})\cdot\left(\Phi((\bm{x}_{i}^{k})^{T}\mathcal{B}_{0})-\Phi((\bm{x}_{i}^{k})^{T}\mathcal{B}^{*})\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\left.\left[h^{\prime\prime}(\eta)\left(\Phi(\eta)-\Phi((\bm{x}_{i}^{k})^{T}\mathcal{B}^{*})\right)+h^{\prime}(\eta)\varphi(\eta)\right]\right|_{\eta=(\bm{x}_{i}^{k})^{T}\mathcal{B}^{**}}\cdot\bm{x}_{i,\mathcal{M}}^{T}(\mathcal{B}_{0,\mathcal{M}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~O(\left[\bm{x}_{i,\mathcal{M}}^{T}(\mathcal{B}_{0,\mathcal{M}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}})\right]^{2})+O(\bm{x}_{i,\mathcal{M}}^{T}(\mathcal{B}_{0,\mathcal{M}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}}))

where the last equality is from the fact that
|h^{\prime\prime}(t)|\leq 1
,
|\Phi(t_{1})-\Phi(t_{2})|\leq\varphi(0)|t_{1}-t_{2}|
, and
h^{\prime}(t)\varphi(t)=\frac{\varphi^{2}}{\Phi(1-\Phi)}(t)\leq\frac{\varphi^{2}}{\Phi(1-\Phi)}(0)
. Along with the 4th condition of (A1), we have
I_{3}/n=o(1/n)
. Therefore,
I_{1}=O((m-r+s+K)/n)
, as desired.

It remains to bound

\mathbb{P}(E_{n}^{c})=\mathbb{P}\left(\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\frac{1}{n}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\left(\hat{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}^{*})-{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}^{*})\right)\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\right\|_{2}\geq\frac{c}{8}\right).

Denote

\displaystyle{\mathcal{R}_{n}}=1+\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\|\bm{x}_{i,\mathcal{M}\cup S}\|_{2}^{2},~T_{n}=\max_{1\leq k\leq K}\lambda_{\max}\left(\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\right)

By the matrix Chernoff bound, we have

\displaystyle~\mathbb{P}\left(\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\left({\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}{\mathcal{B}^{*}})-\hat{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}{\mathcal{B}^{*}})\right)\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\right\|_{2}>t\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~(s+m+K)\exp\left(-\frac{t^{2}}{{\mathcal{R}_{n}}T_{n}+{\mathcal{R}_{n}}t/3}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\exp\left(-\frac{t^{2}}{{\mathcal{R}_{n}}(T_{n}+t/3)}+\log(s+m+K)\right)

(73)

From the 1st and 4th conditions of (A1), we have
{\mathcal{R}_{n}}=O\left({\frac{n}{(s+m+K)\log n}}\right)
,
T_{n}=O(n)
. Then there exists constants
c_{4}
,
c_{5}
such that
{\mathcal{R}_{n}}\leq{\frac{c_{4}n}{(s+m+K)\log n}}
,
T_{n}\leq c_{5}n
for sufficiently large
n
. Thus

\displaystyle~\mathbb{P}\left(\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\frac{1}{n}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\left(\hat{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}^{*})-{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}^{*})\right)\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\right\|_{2}\geq\frac{c}{8}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\exp\left(-\tilde{c}{(s+m+K)\log(n)}+\log(s+m+K)\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\exp\left(-\frac{\tilde{c}}{2}{(s+m+K)\log(n)}\right),

for some constant
\tilde{c}
and sufficiently large
n
.

Step 2: Show that with probability tending to 1, the oracle local maximizer
\hat{\mathcal{B}}
is indeed a maximizer of
Q_{n}(\mathcal{B})
with the linear constraint
\bm{C}\mathcal{B}_{\mathcal{M}}=\bm{t}
.

From step 1, we have for some constant
c
, and any
\tau\ll{\sqrt{\log n}}
,

\mathbb{P}\left(\|\hat{\mathcal{B}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}\|_{2}\leq\tau\sqrt{\frac{m-r+s+K}{n}}\right)\geq 1-c\frac{1}{\tau^{2}}-\exp\left(-\frac{\tilde{c}}{2}{(s+m+K)\log(n)}\right).

Similar to Theorem 1 in
Fan and Lv 2011
, it suffices to show that with probability tending to 1,
\hat{\mathcal{B}}
satisfies the following inequality:

\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}(\bm{X}^{k}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K})^{c}})^{T}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}\hat{\mathcal{B}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}\hat{\mathcal{B}})\right\}\right\|_{\infty}<n\lambda_{n,0}{\rho}^{\prime}(0+).

By the Taylor’s expansion,
\forall j\in(\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K})^{c}
,

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\bm{x}_{(j)}^{T}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}\hat{\mathcal{B}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}\hat{\mathcal{B}})\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\bm{x}_{(j)}^{T}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}{\mathcal{B}^{*}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}{\mathcal{B}^{*}})\right\}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\bm{x}_{(j)}^{T}\hat{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}{\mathcal{B}^{*}})\bm{X}^{k}(\hat{\mathcal{B}}-\mathcal{B}^{*})+R_{j},

(74)

where

R_{j}=\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\hat{\bm{\Delta}}_{{\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K}}}^{T}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\text{diag}\left\{\bm{x}_{(j)}\circ\hat{\Sigma}^{\prime}(\bm{X}^{K}\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{j})\right\}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\hat{\bm{\Delta}}_{{\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K}}},

for some
\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{j}
lying on the line segment joining
\mathcal{B}^{*}
and
\hat{\mathcal{B}}
,
where
\hat{\bm{\Delta}}_{{\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K}}}=\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K}}
. Under the event

\left\{\|\hat{\mathcal{B}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}\|_{2}\leq\tau\sqrt{{(m-r+s+K)}/{n}}\right\},

(75)

we have
\hat{\mathcal{B}}\in\mathcal{N}^{*}
. Along with
\mathcal{B}^{*}\in\mathcal{N}^{*}
, we have
\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{j}\in\mathcal{N}^{*}
. Then under the same event (
75
), it follows from the 5th condition of (A1) that

\displaystyle|R_{j}|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\hat{\bm{\Delta}}_{{\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K}}}^{T}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\text{diag}\left\{|\bm{x}_{(j)}|\circ|\hat{\Sigma}^{\prime}(\bm{X}^{K}\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{j})|\right\}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\hat{\bm{\Delta}}_{{\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K}}},

\displaystyle\leq n\lambda_{\max}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\text{diag}\left\{|\bm{x}_{(j)}|\right\}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\right)\cdot\|\hat{\bm{\Delta}}_{{\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K}}}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq c_{1}n\cdot\tau^{2}{\frac{m-r+s+K}{n}}

\displaystyle=c_{1}\tau^{2}{(m-r+s+K)}.

Therefore, we have

\sup_{j\in(\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K})^{c}}|R_{j}|\leq c_{1}\tau^{2}{(m-r+s+K)}.

(76)

For the second term of the RHS of inequality (
74
),
we can prove using 2nd and 4th conditions of (A1) that

\left\|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\bm{X}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K})^{c}}^{T}\hat{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}{\mathcal{B}^{*}})\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K}}^{k}\right\|_{2,\infty}=O(1).

Therefore, under the same event (
75
), we have

\displaystyle~\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\bm{X}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K})^{c}}^{T}\hat{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}{\mathcal{B}^{*}})\bm{X}^{k}(\hat{\mathcal{B}}-\mathcal{B}^{*})\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\bm{X}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K})^{c}}^{T}\hat{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}{\mathcal{B}^{*}})\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K}}^{k}\right\|_{2,\infty}\cdot\left\|\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K}}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~c_{2}n\cdot\tau\sqrt{\frac{m-r+s+K}{n}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~c_{2}\tau\sqrt{(m-r+s+K)n}.

(77)

For the first term in the RHS of inequality (
74
), by Hoeffding’s inequality, given some constant
\gamma>0
,
\forall j\in(\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K})^{c}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\bm{x}_{(j)}^{T}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}{\mathcal{B}^{*}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}{\mathcal{B}^{*}})\right\}\right|>\gamma\sqrt{n\log p}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2\exp\left(-\frac{2\gamma^{2}n\log p}{\sum_{i=1}^{n}x_{ij}^{2}[\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}h^{\prime}((\bm{x}_{i}^{k})^{T}\mathcal{B}^{*})]^{2}}\right).

By the 2nd and 3rd conditions of (A1) and
|h^{\prime\prime}(\cdot)|\leq 1
, we have

{\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left[\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}|h^{\prime}((\bm{x}_{i}^{k})^{T}\mathcal{B}^{*})\cdot x_{ij}|\right]^{2}}=O(n).

Therefore, combined with the previous inequality, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\bm{x}_{(j)}^{T}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}{\mathcal{B}^{*}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}{\mathcal{B}^{*}})\right\}\right|>\gamma\sqrt{n\log p}\right)\leq 2\exp\left(-\frac{2\gamma^{2}\log p}{M}\right)

for some constant
M
and for
n
sufficiently large. Thus, by Bonferroni’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle~\mathbb{P}\left(\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\bm{X}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K})^{c}}^{T}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}{\mathcal{B}^{*}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}{\mathcal{B}^{8}})\right\}\right\|_{\infty}>\gamma\sqrt{n\log p}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~2\exp\left(-\frac{2\gamma^{2}\log p}{M}+\log(p-m-s)\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~2\exp\left(-\frac{\gamma^{2}\log p}{M}\right),

as long as
\gamma^{2}\geq M
.

Combining with inequalities (
76
), and (
77
), we have under event (
75
) and

\left\{\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\bm{X}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K})^{c}}^{T}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}{\mathcal{B}^{*}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}{\mathcal{B}^{*}})\right\}\right\|_{\infty}\leq\gamma\sqrt{n\log p}\right\},

we have

\displaystyle~\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}(\bm{X}^{k}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K})^{c}})^{T}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}\hat{\mathcal{B}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}\hat{\mathcal{B}})\right\}\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\gamma\sqrt{n\log p}+c_{2}\tau\sqrt{(m-r+s+K)n}+c_{1}\tau^{2}{(m-r+s+K)}

\displaystyle<

\displaystyle~n\lambda_{n,0}{\rho}^{\prime}(0+),

as long as we have
\gamma<\frac{{\rho}^{\prime}(0+)}{3}\lambda_{n,0}\sqrt{{n}/{\log p}}
,
\tau<\frac{{\rho}^{\prime}(0+)}{3c_{2}}\lambda_{n,0}\sqrt{{n}/{(m-r+s+K)}}
, and

\tau^{2}<\frac{{\rho}^{\prime}(0+)}{3c_{1}}{\frac{\lambda_{n,0}n}{(m-r+s+K)}}.

By the 3rd condition of (A2) and
s+m+K=o(\sqrt{n})
, we know that
\lambda_{n,0}\sqrt{{n}/{\log p}}
,
\lambda_{n,0}\sqrt{{n}/{(m-r+s+K)}}
, and
\lambda_{n,0}{{n}/{(m-r+s+K)}}
could be arbitrarily large if
n
is sufficiently large. Therefore, by choosing a diverging sequence of
\{\tau_{n}\}
and a sufficiently large positive constant
\gamma
such that all the previous inequality constraints on
\gamma
and
\tau
hold, we could have

\displaystyle~\mathbb{P}\left(\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}(\bm{X}^{k}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K})^{c}})^{T}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}\hat{\mathcal{B}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}\hat{\mathcal{B}})\right\}\right\|_{\infty}<n\lambda_{n,0}{\rho}^{\prime}(0+).\right)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle~1-c\frac{1}{\tau^{2}}\exp\left(-\frac{\tilde{c}}{2}{(s+m+K)\log(n)}\right)-2\exp\left(-\frac{\gamma^{2}\log p}{M}\right)\rightarrow 1,

as desired.

Step 3: Derive the asymptotic distribution of
\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0}

We have shown in Step 1 and Step 2 that for any
\tau\ll\sqrt{\log n}
and sufficiently large
n
,

\displaystyle\|\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K}}\|_{2}=O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{m-r+s+K}{n}}\right),

(78)

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,(\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K})^{c}}=\bm{0}\right)\rightarrow 1,

(79)

\displaystyle\bm{C}\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,\mathcal{M}}=\bm{t}.

(80)

Finally, we show that

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}\begin{pmatrix}\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,\mathcal{M}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}}\\
\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,S\cup\mathcal{K}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}(\bm{I}-\bm{P}_{n})\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}^{*})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}\mathcal{B}^{*})\right\}

\displaystyle-\sqrt{n}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}\bm{P}_{n}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}(\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T})^{-1}\bm{h}_{n}\\
\bm{0}_{s+K}\end{pmatrix}+o_{p}(1).

From the results of Step 1 and Step 2, we know that with probability tending to 1,
\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0}
is the local maximizer of
Q_{n}(\mathcal{B})
with the constraints
\bm{C}\mathcal{B}_{\mathcal{M}}=\bm{t}
, and
\mathcal{B}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K})^{c}}=\bm{0}
. This implies that there exists some vector
\bm{v}\in\mathbb{R}^{r}
such that

\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
\bm{X}_{S}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}{\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}{\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0}})\right\}=\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{n}\bm{C}^{T}\bm{v}\\
n\lambda_{n,0}\bar{\rho}({\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,S}})\\
\bm{0}_{K}\end{pmatrix}

(81)

By Taylor’s expansion, the LHS of equation (
81
) is equal to

\displaystyle~\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\right\}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle~\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}{\mathcal{B}^{*}})\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\begin{pmatrix}\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,\mathcal{M}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}}\\
\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,S\cup\mathcal{K}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle~\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\left({\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}{\mathcal{B}^{*}})-\hat{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}{\mathcal{B}^{*}})\right)\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\begin{pmatrix}\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,\mathcal{M}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}}\\
\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,S\cup\mathcal{K}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}}\end{pmatrix}+\bm{R},

(82)

where

R_{j}=\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\hat{\Delta}_{0,\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K}}^{T}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\text{diag}\left\{\bm{x}^{k}_{(j)}\circ\hat{\Sigma}^{\prime}(\bm{X}^{K}\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{j})\right\}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\hat{\Delta}_{0,\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K}},

for some
\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{j}
lying on the line segment joining
\mathcal{B}^{*}
and
\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0}
, and
\hat{\Delta}_{0,\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K}}=\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K}}
. For
j\in\mathcal{M}\cup S
,

\displaystyle|R_{j}|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\hat{\Delta}_{0,\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K}}^{T}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\text{diag}\left\{|\bm{x}_{(j)}|\circ|\hat{\Sigma}^{\prime}(\bm{X}^{K}\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{j})|\right\}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\hat{\Delta}_{0,\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K}},

\displaystyle\leq n\lambda_{\max}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\text{diag}\left\{|\bm{x}_{(j)}|\right\}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\right)\cdot\|\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K}}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=O(n)\cdot O_{p}\left(\frac{m-r+s+K}{n}\right),

where the last equality is by the 5th condition of (A1) and result (
78
) proved in step 1 and 2. Similarly, for
j\in\mathcal{K}
, we have
\bm{x}^{k}_{(j)}=-\mathbf{1}_{n}\mathbf{I}\{k=j\}
. By the 4th condition of (A1), we have

\displaystyle|R_{j}|

\displaystyle\leq w_{j}\lambda_{\max}\left(\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{j}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{I}_{n}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{j}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\right)\cdot\|\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K}}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=O(n)\cdot O_{p}\left(\frac{m-r+s+K}{n}\right).

Therefore, we have

\|\bm{R}\|_{2}\leq\sqrt{s+m+K}\|\bm{R}\|_{\infty}=O_{p}\left(\sqrt{(s+m+K)^{3}}\right)=o_{p}(\sqrt{n}),

(83)

where the last equality is because we assume that
s+m+K=o(n^{1/3})
.

Next we show that

\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\left({\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}{\mathcal{B}^{*}})-\hat{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}{\mathcal{B}^{*}})\right)\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\begin{pmatrix}\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,\mathcal{M}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}}\\
\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,S\cup\mathcal{K}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}}\end{pmatrix}\right\|_{2}=o_{p}(\sqrt{n}).

(84)

Since
\|\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K}}\|_{2}=O_{p}(\sqrt{(m-r+s+K)/n})
, it suffices to show that

\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\left({\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}{\mathcal{B}^{*}})-\hat{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}{\mathcal{B}^{*}})\right)\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\right\|_{2}=o_{p}\left(\frac{n}{\sqrt{s+m+K}}\right).

By the matrix Chernoff bound (
73
) in step 1, we have

\displaystyle~\mathbb{P}\left(\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\left({\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}{\mathcal{B}^{*}})-\hat{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}{\mathcal{B}^{*}})\right)\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\right\|_{2}>\tau\frac{n}{\sqrt{s+m+K}}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{{\log(s+m+K)}}{{\log n}}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\exp\left(-\frac{\tau^{2}\frac{n^{2}}{s+m+K}\frac{\log(s+m+K)}{{\log n}}}{{\frac{c_{4}n}{(s+m+K)\log n}}\left(c_{5}n+\frac{\tau}{3}\frac{n}{\sqrt{s+m+K}}\sqrt{\frac{{\log(s+m+K)}}{{\log n}}}\right)}+\log(s+m+K)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\exp\left(-\frac{\tau^{2}\log(s+m+K)}{c_{4}\left(c_{5}+\frac{\tau}{3}\sqrt{\frac{{\log(s+m+K)}}{{(s+m+K)\log n}}}\right)}+\log(s+m+K)\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\exp\left(-\frac{\tau^{2}\log(s+m+K)}{c_{4}\left(c_{5}+\frac{\tau}{3}c_{6}\right)}+\log(s+m+K)\right),

where the last inequality is because
\sqrt{\frac{{\log(s+m+K)}}{{(s+m+K)\log n}}}=o(1)
and
c_{6}>0
is some constant. The RHS of the above inequality could be arbitrarily small as long as
\tau
is sufficiently large and
n
is sufficiently large. Therefore, we proved that

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\left({\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}{\mathcal{B}^{*}})-\hat{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}{\mathcal{B}^{*}})\right)\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{n}{\sqrt{s+m+K}}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{{\log(s+m+K)}}{{\log n}}}\right)

\displaystyle=o_{p}\left(\frac{n}{\sqrt{s+m+K}}\right),

(85)

as desired.

Combining equations (
82
), (
83
), and (
84
), we have

\displaystyle~\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}{\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}{\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0}})\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\right\}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle~\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}{\mathcal{B}^{*}})\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\begin{pmatrix}\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,\mathcal{M}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}}\\
\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,S\cup\mathcal{K}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}}\end{pmatrix}+o_{p}(\sqrt{n}).

(86)

Combining equations (
81
) and (
86
), we have

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{n}\bm{C}^{T}\bm{v}\\
n\lambda_{n,0}\bar{\rho}({\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,S}})\\
\bm{0}_{K}\end{pmatrix}=

\displaystyle~\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\right\}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle~\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}{\mathcal{B}^{*}})\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\begin{pmatrix}\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,\mathcal{M}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}}\\
\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,S\cup\mathcal{K}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}}\end{pmatrix}+o_{p}(\sqrt{n}).

(87)

Define that

\bm{K}_{n}=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}{\mathcal{B}^{*}})\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}

Under the 6th condition of (A1), we have
\lim\inf_{n}\lambda_{\min}(\bm{K}_{n})>0
. This implies
\|\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}\bm{R}\|_{2}=o_{p}(\sqrt{n})
. Combining this together with equation (
87
), we have

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}\begin{pmatrix}\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,\mathcal{M}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}}\\
\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,S\cup\mathcal{K}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\right\}

\displaystyle-\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\bm{v}\\
\sqrt{n}\lambda_{n,0}\bar{\rho}({\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,S}})\\
\bm{0}_{K}\end{pmatrix}+o_{p}(1)

(88)

Since
\|\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K}}\|_{2}=O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{m-r+s+K}{n}}\right)

and
d_{n}\gg\sqrt{(s+m+K)/n}
by the 3rd condition of (A2),
we have
\|\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K}}\|_{\infty}\leq\|\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K}}\|_{2}=o_{p}(d_{n})
.
Thus, with probability tending to
1
,

\min_{j\in{S}}|\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,j}|\geq\min_{j\in{S}}|{\mathcal{B}^{*}_{j}}|-\|\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K}}\|_{\infty}>d_{n}.

By the monotonicity of
\rho^{\prime}
and the 2nd condition of (A2), we obtain

\|\sqrt{n}\lambda_{n,0}\bar{\rho}({\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,S}})\|_{2}\leq\sqrt{s}\sqrt{n}\lambda_{n,0}|\rho^{\prime}(d_{n})|=o(1),

with probability tending to 1. This together with equation (
88
) suggests that

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}\begin{pmatrix}\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,\mathcal{M}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}}\\
\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,S\cup\mathcal{K}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\right\}

\displaystyle-\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{v}+o_{p}(1).

(89)

Since
\bm{C}\mathcal{B}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}}-\bm{t}=\bm{h}_{n}
, we have
\bm{C}(\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,\mathcal{M}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}})=-\bm{h}_{n}
. Therefore, multiplying both sides of equation (
89
) by

\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}&\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}\end{pmatrix}
,
we have

\displaystyle~\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{v}-\sqrt{n}\bm{h}_{n}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\right\}+\bm{C}\bm{R}^{*}

(90)

for some
m
-dimensional vector
\bm{R}^{*}
such that
\|\bm{R}^{*}\|_{2}=o_{p}(1)
. Define
\Omega_{n}=\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}
and
\Omega_{mm}
is the
m\times m
top-left block of
\Omega_{n}
. By multiplying
{(\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T})^{-1}}
on both sides of equation (
90
), we have

\displaystyle\bm{v}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}{(\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T})^{-1}}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\right\}

\displaystyle+{(\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T})^{-1}}\bm{C}\bm{R}^{*}+\sqrt{n}{(\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T})^{-1}}\bm{h}_{n}.

This together with equation (
89
) yields

\displaystyle~\sqrt{n}\begin{pmatrix}\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,\mathcal{M}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}}\\
\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,S\cup\mathcal{K}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}}\end{pmatrix}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\right\}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle~\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}{(\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T})^{-1}}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\right\}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle~\sqrt{n}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}{(\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T})^{-1}}\bm{h}_{n}-\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}{(\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T})^{-1}}\bm{C}\bm{R}^{*}+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}(\bm{I}-\bm{P}_{n})\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\right\}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle~\sqrt{n}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}{(\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T})^{-1}}\bm{h}_{n}-\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
