\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}{(\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T})^{-1}}\bm{C}\bm{R}^{*}+o_{p}(1),

(91)

where
\bm{P}_{n}
is a
(m+s+K)\times(m+s+K)
projection matrix of rank
r
.

\bm{P}_{n}=\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}\left(\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}\right)^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}.

Next, we show that

\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}{(\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T})^{-1}}\bm{C}\bm{R}^{*}=o_{p}(1)

(92)

Under the 4th condition in (A1), we have
\lambda_{\max}(\bm{K}_{n})=O(1)
. This implies that
\lim\inf_{n}\lambda_{\min}(\bm{K}_{n}^{-1})>0
. Thus
\lim\inf_{n}\lambda_{\min}(\Omega_{mm})>0
. Similarly, by the 6th condition in (A1), we have
\lim\inf_{n}\lambda_{\min}(\bm{K}_{n})>0
, which implies that
\lambda_{\max}(\Omega_{mm})=O(1)
. Therefore, using Cauchy-Schwartz inequality, we have

\displaystyle~\left\|\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}{(\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T})^{-1}}\bm{C}\bm{R}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\|\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}\|_{2}^{2}\cdot\left\|\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}{(\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T})^{-1}}\bm{C}\right\|_{2}^{2}\cdot\|\bm{R}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\lambda_{\max}(\bm{K}_{n}^{-1})\cdot\left\|\bm{C}^{T}{(\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T})^{-1}}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}{(\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T})^{-1}}\bm{C}\right\|_{2}\cdot\|\bm{R}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\lambda_{\max}(\bm{K}_{n}^{-1})\cdot\left\|\bm{C}^{T}{(\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T})^{-1}}\bm{C}\right\|_{2}\cdot\|\bm{R}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\lambda_{\max}(\bm{K}_{n}^{-1})\cdot\left\|{(\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T})^{-1/2}}\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}^{1/2}\cdot\Omega_{mm}^{-1/2}\right\|_{2}^{2}\cdot\|\bm{R}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\lambda_{\max}(\bm{K}_{n}^{-1})\cdot\left\|{(\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T})^{-1/2}}\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}^{1/2}\right\|_{2}^{2}\cdot\|\Omega_{mm}^{-1/2}\|_{2}^{2}\cdot\|\bm{R}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\lambda_{\max}(\bm{K}_{n}^{-1})\cdot\|\bm{I}_{r}\|_{2}\cdot\lambda_{\max}(\Omega_{mm}^{-1})\cdot\|\bm{R}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}=o_{p}(1).

This proves equation (
92
). Hence, it follows from equation (
91
) that

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}\begin{pmatrix}\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,\mathcal{M}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}}\\
\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,S\cup\mathcal{K}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}(\bm{I}-\bm{P}_{n})\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\right\}

\displaystyle-\sqrt{n}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}{(\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T})^{-1}}\bm{h}_{n}+o_{p}(1).

(93)

Moreover, observe the following equality

\displaystyle\bm{h}_{n}

\displaystyle={\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T}}({\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T}})^{-1}\bm{h}_{n}

\displaystyle=\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}({\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T}})^{-1}\bm{h}_{n}

\displaystyle=\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}({\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T}})^{-1}\bm{h}_{n}\\
\bm{0}_{s+K}\end{pmatrix}.

Hence, we have

\displaystyle~\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}{(\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T})^{-1}}\bm{h}_{n}

\displaystyle=\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}{(\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T})^{-1}}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}({\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T}})^{-1}\bm{h}_{n}\\
\bm{0}_{s+K}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle=\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}\bm{P}_{n}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}({\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T}})^{-1}\bm{h}_{n}\\
\bm{0}_{s+K}\end{pmatrix}

(94)

This together with equation (
94
) implies that

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}\begin{pmatrix}\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,\mathcal{M}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}}\\
\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,S\cup\mathcal{K}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}(\bm{I}-\bm{P}_{n})\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\right\}

\displaystyle-\sqrt{n}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}\bm{P}_{n}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}({\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T}})^{-1}\bm{h}_{n}\\
\bm{0}_{s+K}\end{pmatrix}+o_{p}(1).

(95)

Similarly, one can prove that

\sqrt{n}\begin{pmatrix}\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a,\mathcal{M}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}}\\
\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a,S\cup\mathcal{K}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}}\end{pmatrix}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{8}}})\right\}+o_{p}(1).

(96)

∎

A.3
Proof of Theorem
2

Proof.

Define

T_{0}=({\bm{\omega}_{n}+\sqrt{n}\bm{h}_{n}})^{T}({\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T}})^{-1}({\bm{\omega}_{n}+\sqrt{n}\bm{h}_{n}})

and

\bm{\omega}_{n}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\right\}.

Step 1: Show that
T_{W}/r
is equivalent to
T_{0}/r
.

By Theorem 2.1,

\sqrt{n}\begin{pmatrix}\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a,\mathcal{M}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}}\\
\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a,S\cup\mathcal{K}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}}\end{pmatrix}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\right\}+\bm{R}_{a},

where
\bm{R}_{a}
is a
(m+s+K)
-dimensional vector satisfying
\|\bm{R}_{a}\|_{2}=o_{p}(1)
. Therefore, by multiplying both sides of the above equation by
\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}&\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}\end{pmatrix}
, we have

\sqrt{n}\bm{C}(\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a,\mathcal{M}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}})=\bm{\omega}_{n}+\bm{C}\bm{R}_{a,J_{0}},

(97)

where
J_{0}=[1,\dots,m]
. Since
\bm{C}\mathcal{B}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}}=\bm{t}+\bm{h}_{n}
, it follow from equation (
97
) that

\sqrt{n}(\bm{C}\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a,\mathcal{M}}-\bm{t})=\bm{\omega}_{n}+\bm{C}\bm{R}_{a,J_{0}}+\sqrt{n}\bm{h}_{n}

and hence

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}({\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T}})^{-1/2}(\bm{C}\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a,\mathcal{M}}-\bm{t})

\displaystyle=({\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T}})^{-1/2}(\bm{\omega}_{n}+\bm{C}\bm{R}_{a,J_{0}}+\sqrt{n}\bm{h}_{n})

\displaystyle=({\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T}})^{-1/2}(\bm{\omega}_{n}+\sqrt{n}\bm{h}_{n})+({\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T}})^{-1/2}\bm{C}\bm{R}_{a,J_{0}}

(98)

by the 6th condition in (A1), we have
\lim\inf_{n}\lambda_{\min}(\bm{K}_{n})>0
, which implies that
\lambda_{\max}(\Omega_{mm})=O(1)
.
Then we can prove that
\|({\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T}})^{-1/2}\bm{C}\|_{2}=O(1)
,

\displaystyle\|({\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T}})^{-1/2}\bm{C}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\|({\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T}})^{-1/2}\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}^{1/2}\cdot\Omega_{mm}^{1/2}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|({\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T}})^{-1/2}\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}^{1/2}\|_{2}^{2}\|\Omega_{mm}^{1/2}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\lambda_{\max}\left(({\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T}})^{-1/2}{\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T}}({\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T}})^{-1/2}\right)\cdot\|\Omega_{mm}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\lambda_{\max}(\bm{I}_{r})\|\Omega_{mm}\|_{2}=O(1).

This implies that

\|({\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T}})^{-1/2}\bm{C}\bm{R}_{a,J_{0}}\|_{2}\leq\|({\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T}})^{-1/2}\bm{C}\|_{2}\|\bm{R}_{a,J_{0}}\|_{2}=o_{p}(1).

This together with equation (
98
) gives

\sqrt{n}({\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T}})^{-1/2}(\bm{C}\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a,\mathcal{M}}-\bm{t})=({\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T}})^{-1/2}(\bm{\omega}_{n}+\sqrt{n}\bm{h}_{n})+o_{p}(1).

(99)

Next, show that

\|({\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T}})^{-1/2}(\bm{\omega}_{n}+\sqrt{n}\bm{h}_{n})\|_{2}=O_{p}(\sqrt{r}).

(100)

To prove
\|({\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T}})^{-1/2}\bm{\omega}_{n}\|_{2}=O_{p}(\sqrt{r})
, we notice that

\displaystyle~\|({\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T}})^{-1/2}\bm{\omega}_{n}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\right\}\right)^{T}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}\cdot

\displaystyle~\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}({\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T}})^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}\cdot

\displaystyle~\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\right\}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\left\|\bm{P}_{n}^{1/2}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\right\}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}.

Since

\displaystyle~\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\bm{P}_{n}^{1/2}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\right\}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\frac{1}{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\left.\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\bm{P}_{n}^{1/2}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\right\}\right.\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\left.\bm{P}_{n}^{1/2}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\right\}\right.\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\text{tr}\left[\bm{P}_{n}^{1/2}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}\bm{P}_{n}^{1/2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\text{tr}\left[\bm{P}_{n}^{1/2}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\right)\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}\bm{P}_{n}^{1/2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\text{tr}\left[\bm{P}_{n}^{1/2}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}\bm{K}_{n}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}\bm{P}_{n}^{1/2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\text{tr}\left[\bm{P}_{n}\right]=r,

where the first inequality is by the convexity of
\|\cdot\|_{2}^{2}
, and
\text{tr}\left[\bm{P}_{n}\right]=r
is because
\bm{P}_{n}
is the projection matrix of rank
r
. By Markov’s inequality, we have

\left\|\bm{P}_{n}^{1/2}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\right\}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}=O_{p}(r).

Therefore, we proved that
\|({\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T}})^{-1/2}\bm{\omega}_{n}\|_{2}=O_{p}(\sqrt{r})
.
Besides, we can show that
\|({\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T}})^{-1/2}\|_{2}=O(1)
:

\displaystyle\lambda_{\min}({\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T}})

\displaystyle=\inf_{\bm{a}\in\mathbb{R}^{r},\|\bm{a}\|_{2}=1}\bm{a}^{T}{\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T}}\bm{a}

\displaystyle\geq\inf_{\bm{a}\in\mathbb{R}^{r},\|\bm{a}\|_{2}=1}\lambda_{\min}({\Omega_{mm}})\cdot\bm{a}^{T}{\bm{C}\bm{C}^{T}}\bm{a}

\displaystyle\geq\lambda_{\min}({\Omega_{n}})\cdot\lambda_{\min}({\bm{C}\bm{C}^{T}}).

Under the 4th condition in (A1), we have
\lambda_{\max}(\bm{K}_{n})=O(1)
. This implies that
\liminf_{n}\lambda_{\min}(\bm{K}_{n}^{-1})>0
. And condition (A3):
\lambda_{\max}(({\bm{C}\bm{C}^{T}})^{-1})=O(1)
implies that
\liminf_{n}\lambda_{\min}({\bm{C}\bm{C}^{T}})>0
. Thus,
\lim\inf_{n}\lambda_{\min}({\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T}})>0
, or equivalently,
\|({\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T}})^{-1/2}\|_{2}=O(1)
. This together with condition (A3)
\|\bm{h}_{n}\|_{2}=O(\sqrt{r/n})
implies that

\|\sqrt{n}({\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T}})^{-1/2}\bm{h}_{n}\|_{2}=O(\sqrt{r}).

Thus equation (
100
) is proved as desired. Along with (
99
) implies that

\|\sqrt{n}({\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T}})^{-1/2}(\bm{C}\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a,\mathcal{M}}-\bm{t})\|_{2}=O_{p}(\sqrt{r}).

(101)

By Lemma
2
, we have

\left\|\bm{I}-\Psi_{n}^{1/2}({\bm{C}\widehat{\Omega}_{a,mm}\bm{C}^{T}})^{-1}\Psi_{n}^{1/2}\right\|_{2}=O_{p}\left(\frac{s+m+K}{\sqrt{n}}\right),

(102)

where
\Psi_{n}={\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T}}
,
then we will have

\displaystyle~\left|n(\bm{C}\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a,\mathcal{M}}-\bm{t})^{T}({\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T}})^{-1}(\bm{C}\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a,\mathcal{M}}-\bm{t})-n(\bm{C}\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a,\mathcal{M}}-\bm{t})^{T}({\bm{C}\widehat{\Omega}_{a,mm}\bm{C}^{T}})^{-1}(\bm{C}\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a,\mathcal{M}}-\bm{t})\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\left|n(\bm{C}\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a,\mathcal{M}}-\bm{t})^{T}\Psi_{n}^{-1/2}\left(\bm{I}-\Psi_{n}^{1/2}({\bm{C}\widehat{\Omega}_{a,mm}\bm{C}^{T}})^{-1}\Psi_{n}^{1/2}\right)\Psi_{n}^{-1/2}(\bm{C}\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a,\mathcal{M}}-\bm{t})\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\left\|\bm{I}-\Psi_{n}^{1/2}({\bm{C}\widehat{\Omega}_{a,mm}\bm{C}^{T}})^{-1}\Psi_{n}^{1/2}\right\|_{2}\left\|\sqrt{n}\Psi_{n}^{-1/2}(\bm{C}\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a,\mathcal{M}}-\bm{t})\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~O_{p}\left(\frac{s+m+K}{\sqrt{n}}\right)\cdot O_{p}(\sqrt{r})=o_{p}(r),

where the last equality is because the assumption that
s+m+K=o(n^{1/3})
. Therefore, by the definition of
T_{W}
, we have

\displaystyle T_{W}

\displaystyle=n(\bm{C}\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a,\mathcal{M}}-\bm{t})^{T}({\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T}})^{-1}(\bm{C}\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a,\mathcal{M}}-\bm{t})+o_{p}(r)

\displaystyle=T_{0}+o_{p}({r}).

Step 2: Show that
T_{S}/r
is equivalent to
T_{0}/r
.

Based on the proof of Theorem 2.1, we have:

\displaystyle~\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}{\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}{\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0}})\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\right\}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle~\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}{\mathcal{B}^{*}})\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\begin{pmatrix}\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,\mathcal{M}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}}\\
\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,S\cup\mathcal{K}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}}\end{pmatrix}+o_{p}(1).

(103)

and

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}\begin{pmatrix}\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,\mathcal{M}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}}\\
\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,S\cup\mathcal{K}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}(\bm{I}-\bm{P}_{n})\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\right\}

\displaystyle-\sqrt{n}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}{(\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T})^{-1}}\bm{h}_{n}+o_{p}(1).

(104)

Since
\lambda_{\max}(\bm{K}_{n})=O(1)
, we have

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}\bm{K}_{n}\begin{pmatrix}\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,\mathcal{M}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}}\\
\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,S\cup\mathcal{K}}-\mathcal{B}^{*}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\bm{K}_{n}^{1/2}(\bm{I}-\bm{P}_{n})\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\right\}

\displaystyle-\sqrt{n}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}{(\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T})^{-1}}\bm{h}_{n}+o_{p}(1),

(105)

The LHS of above equation is the same as the second term in the RHS of equation (
103
). Thus, this together with (
103
) implies that

\displaystyle~\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}{\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}{\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0}})\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\right\}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle~\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\bm{K}_{n}^{1/2}(\bm{I}-\bm{P}_{n})\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\right\}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle~\sqrt{n}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}{(\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T})^{-1}}\bm{h}_{n}+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\bm{K}_{n}^{1/2}\bm{P}_{n}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\right\}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle~\sqrt{n}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}{(\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T})^{-1}}\bm{h}_{n}+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}{(\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T})^{-1}}(\bm{\omega_{n}}+\sqrt{n}\bm{h}_{n})+o_{p}(1),

(106)

where the last equality is by the definition of
\bm{P}_{n}
and
\bm{\omega}_{n}
.
Multiplying both sides by
\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}
and from the fact that
\lim\inf_{n}\lambda_{\min}(\bm{K}_{n})>0
, we have

\displaystyle~\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}{\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}{\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0}})\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}{(\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T})^{-1}}(\bm{\omega_{n}}+\sqrt{n}\bm{h}_{n})+o_{p}(1),

(107)

We proved in Step 1 that
\|({\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T}})^{-1/2}(\bm{\omega_{n}}+\sqrt{n}\bm{h}_{n})\|_{2}=O_{p}(\sqrt{r})
, thus

\left\|\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}{(\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T})^{-1}}(\bm{\omega_{n}}+\sqrt{n}\bm{h}_{n})\right\|_{2}^{2}=\|({\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T}})^{-1/2}(\bm{\omega_{n}}+\sqrt{n}\bm{h}_{n})\|_{2}^{2}=O_{p}({r}).

This implies that

\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}{\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}{\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0}})\right\}\right\|_{2}=O_{p}({\sqrt{r}}).

By Lemma
2
, we have

\left\|\bm{I}-\Omega_{n}^{-1/2}\widehat{\Omega}_{0}\Omega_{n}^{-1/2}\right\|_{2}=O_{p}\left(\frac{s+m+K}{\sqrt{n}}\right).

Then under event
\{\widehat{S}_{0}=S\}
, we have

\displaystyle~\left|\frac{1}{n}\mathcal{S}_{n}(\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0})^{T}(\Omega_{n}-\widehat{\Omega}_{0})\mathcal{S}_{n}(\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0})\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{\widehat{S}_{0}\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}{\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}{\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0}})\right\}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left\|\bm{I}-\Omega_{n}^{-1/2}\widehat{\Omega}_{0}\Omega_{n}^{-1/2}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}{\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}{\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0}})\right\}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left\|\bm{I}-\Omega_{n}^{-1/2}\widehat{\Omega}_{0}\Omega_{n}^{-1/2}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~O_{p}\left(\frac{r(s+m+K)}{\sqrt{n}}\right)=o_{p}(r).

Since
\mathbb{P}(\widehat{S}_{0}=S)\rightarrow 1
and
s+m+K=o(\sqrt{n})
, we have

T_{S}=T_{0}+o_{p}(r).

Step 3: Show that
T_{L}/r
is equivalent to
T_{0}/r
.

By Theorem 2.1, we have

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}\begin{pmatrix}\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a,\mathcal{M}}-\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,\mathcal{M}}\\
\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a,S\cup\mathcal{K}}-\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,S\cup\mathcal{K}}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}{\bm{P}_{n}}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\right\}

\displaystyle+\sqrt{n}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}\bm{P}_{n}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}({\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T}})^{-1}\bm{h}_{n}\\
\bm{0}_{s+K}\end{pmatrix}+o_{p}(1).

Notice that

\displaystyle~\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}\bm{P}_{n}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}({\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T}})^{-1}\bm{h}_{n}\\
\bm{0}_{s+K}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}({\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T}})^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}({\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T}})^{-1}\bm{h}_{n}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}({\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T}})^{-1}\bm{h}_{n}.

It follows that

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}\begin{pmatrix}\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a,\mathcal{M}}-\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,\mathcal{M}}\\
\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a,S\cup\mathcal{K}}-\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,S\cup\mathcal{K}}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}{\bm{P}_{n}}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\right\}

\displaystyle+\sqrt{n}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}({\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T}})^{-1}\bm{h}_{n}+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}({\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T}})^{-1}(\bm{\omega}_{n}+\sqrt{n}\bm{h}_{n})+o_{p}(1).

(108)

We proved in Step 1 that
\|({\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T}})^{-1/2}(\bm{\omega_{n}}+\sqrt{n}\bm{h}_{n})\|_{2}=O_{p}(\sqrt{r})
, thus

\displaystyle~\left\|\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}{(\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T})^{-1}}(\bm{\omega_{n}}+\sqrt{n}\bm{h}_{n})\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\left\|\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left\|\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}{(\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T})^{-1}}(\bm{\omega_{n}}+\sqrt{n}\bm{h}_{n})\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\lambda_{\max}(\bm{K}_{n}^{-1})\left\|{(\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T})^{-1/2}}(\bm{\omega_{n}}+\sqrt{n}\bm{h}_{n})\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~O_{p}({r}).

(109)

Thus, we proved that

\left\|\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a,\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K}}-\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K}}\right\|_{2}=O_{p}({\sqrt{r/n}})

(110)

Under the event
\left\{\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a,(\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K})^{c}}=\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,(\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K})^{c}}=\bm{0}\right\}
, denote
\hat{\Delta}_{a,0}=\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a,\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K}}-\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K}}
. By the second-order Taylor expansion, we obtain that

\displaystyle M_{n}(\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a})-M_{n}(\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\hat{\Delta}_{a,0}^{T}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}{\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}{\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a}})\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2n}\hat{\Delta}_{a,0}^{T}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\hat{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}{\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a}})\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\hat{\Delta}_{a,0}+\frac{1}{n}\hat{\Delta}_{a,0}^{T}\bm{R},

(111)

where
\bm{R}
satisfies
\|\bm{R}\|_{2}=O_{p}(r\sqrt{s+m+K})
. Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{n}\hat{\Delta}_{a,0}^{T}\bm{R}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}\left\|\hat{\Delta}_{a,0}\right\|_{2}\left\|\bm{R}\right\|_{2}=O_{p}\left(\frac{\sqrt{r}}{n}\cdot\frac{r\sqrt{s+m+K}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)=o_{p}\left(\frac{{\sqrt{r}}}{n}\right),

(112)

where the last equality is because
(s+m+K)=o(n^{1/3})
. Notice that

\hat{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}{\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a}})=(\hat{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}{\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a}})-\hat{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}}))+(\hat{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})-{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}}))+{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}}).

By Cauchy Schwartz inequality,

\displaystyle~\left|\frac{1}{n}\hat{\Delta}_{a,0}^{T}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}(\hat{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}{\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a}})-\hat{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\hat{\Delta}_{a,0}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\left\|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}(\hat{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}{\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a}})-\hat{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\right\|_{2}\left\|\hat{\Delta}_{a,0}\right\|_{2}^{2}.

By the the 4th condition of (A1) and
\sup_{t}|\hat{\Sigma}^{\prime}(t)|=O_{p}(1)
, we have

\displaystyle~\left\|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}(\hat{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}{\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a}})-\hat{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\left\|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\hat{\Sigma}^{\prime}(\bm{X}^{k}{\bar{\mathcal{B}}})\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\hat{\Delta}_{a,0}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\left\|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\sup_{t}|\hat{\Sigma}^{\prime}(t)|\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\right\|_{2}\left\|\hat{\Delta}_{a,0}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~O_{p}(1)\cdot O_{p}\left(\sqrt{{r}/{n}}\right).

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle~\left|\frac{1}{n}\hat{\Delta}_{a,0}^{T}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}(\hat{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}{\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a}})-\hat{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\hat{\Delta}_{a,0}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~O_{p}(\sqrt{r/n})\cdot O_{p}(r/n)=o_{p}(\sqrt{r}/n).

(113)

Next, show that

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{n}\hat{\Delta}_{a,0}^{T}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}(\hat{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})-{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}}))\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\hat{\Delta}_{a,0}\right|=o_{p}\left(\frac{{\sqrt{r}}}{n}\right).

(114)

When proving the asymptotic distribution in Theorem 2.1 (
85
), we proved that

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\left({\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}{\mathcal{B}^{*}})-\hat{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}{\mathcal{B}^{*}})\right)\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{n}{\sqrt{s+m+K}}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{{\log(s+m+K)}}{{\log n}}}\right).

This implies that

\displaystyle~\left|\frac{1}{n}\hat{\Delta}_{a,0}^{T}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}(\hat{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})-{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}}))\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\hat{\Delta}_{a,0}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\frac{1}{n}\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\left({\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}{\mathcal{B}^{*}})-\hat{\Sigma}(\bm{X}^{k}{\mathcal{B}^{*}})\right)\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\right\|_{2}\cdot\left\|\hat{\Delta}_{a,0}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~O_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{s+m+K}}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{{\log(s+m+K)}}{{\log n}}}\right)\cdot O_{p}\left({\frac{r}{n}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~O_{p}\left(\frac{\sqrt{r}}{n}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{r}{s+m+K}}\sqrt{\frac{{\log(s+m+K)}}{{\log n}}}\right)=o_{p}\left(\frac{\sqrt{r}}{n}\right).

where the last equality is because
r\leq s+m+K
and
s+m+K=o(\sqrt{n})
. Combining (
111
), (
112
), (
113
), and (
114
), we have under the event
\left\{\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a,(\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K})^{c}}=\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,(\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K})^{c}}=\bm{0}\right\}
,

\displaystyle n\left[M_{n}(\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a})-M_{n}(\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0})\right]

\displaystyle=\begin{pmatrix}\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a,\mathcal{M}}-\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,\mathcal{M}}\\
\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a,S\cup\mathcal{K}}-\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,S\cup\mathcal{K}}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}{\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}{\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a}})\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{n}{2}\begin{pmatrix}\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a,\mathcal{M}}-\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,\mathcal{M}}\\
\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a,S\cup\mathcal{K}}-\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,S\cup\mathcal{K}}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\bm{K}_{n}\begin{pmatrix}\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a,\mathcal{M}}-\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,\mathcal{M}}\\
\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a,S\cup\mathcal{K}}-\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,S\cup\mathcal{K}}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle+o_{p}\left(\sqrt{r}\right).

(115)

Next show that

\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}{\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}{\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a}})\right\}\right\|_{2}=o_{p}(\sqrt{n})

(116)

By Theorem 2.1, we have with probability tending to 1 that
\min_{j\in S}|\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a,j}|\geq d_{n}
. By the first-order optimality condition of
\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a}
, we have

\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}{\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}{\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a}})\right\}=\begin{pmatrix}\bm{0}_{m}\\
n\lambda_{n,a}\bar{\rho}(\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a,S)},\lambda_{n,a})\\
\bm{0}_{K}\end{pmatrix}.

Under the 2nd condition of (A2) that
p^{\prime}_{\lambda_{n,a}}(d_{n})=o(1/\sqrt{(s+m)n})
, we have

\|n\lambda_{n,a}\bar{\rho}(\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a,S\cup\mathcal{K})},\lambda_{n,a})\|_{2}\leq n\sqrt{s}|p^{\prime}_{\lambda_{n,a}}(d_{n})|=o(\sqrt{n}).

This proves equation (
116
) as desired. Along with (
110
), we have

\left|\begin{pmatrix}\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a,\mathcal{M}}-\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,\mathcal{M}}\\
\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a,S\cup\mathcal{K}}-\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,S\cup\mathcal{K}}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}{\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}{\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a}})\right\}\right|=o_{p}(\sqrt{r}).

(117)

As a result, we have under the event
\left\{\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a,(\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K})^{c}}=\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,(\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K})^{c}}=\bm{0}\right\}
,

\displaystyle 2n\left[M_{n}(\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a})-M_{n}(\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0})\right]

\displaystyle={n}\begin{pmatrix}\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a,\mathcal{M}}-\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,\mathcal{M}}\\
\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a,S\cup\mathcal{K}}-\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,S\cup\mathcal{K}}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\bm{K}_{n}\begin{pmatrix}\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a,\mathcal{M}}-\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,\mathcal{M}}\\
\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a,S\cup\mathcal{K}}-\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,S\cup\mathcal{K}}\end{pmatrix}+o_{p}\left(\sqrt{r}\right).

(118)

From the fact that
\left\|{(\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T})^{-1/2}}(\bm{\omega_{n}}+\sqrt{n}\bm{h}_{n})\right\|_{2}=O_{p}(\sqrt{r})
and
\lambda_{\max}(\bm{K}_{n})=O(1)
, we have

\displaystyle~\left\|\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}{(\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T})^{-1}}(\bm{\omega_{n}}+\sqrt{n}\bm{h}_{n})\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\left\|\bm{K}_{n}^{1/2}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}{(\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T})^{-1}}(\bm{\omega_{n}}+\sqrt{n}\bm{h}_{n})\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\|\bm{K}_{n}^{1/2}\|_{2}^{2}\left\|\bm{K}_{n}^{-1/2}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}{(\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T})^{-1}}(\bm{\omega_{n}}+\sqrt{n}\bm{h}_{n})\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\|\bm{K}_{n}\|_{2}\left\|{(\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T})^{-1/2}}(\bm{\omega_{n}}+\sqrt{n}\bm{h}_{n})\right\|_{2}^{2}=O_{p}(r)

In view of equation (
108
), we have

\displaystyle~\left|{n}\begin{pmatrix}\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a,\mathcal{M}}-\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,\mathcal{M}}\\
\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a,S\cup\mathcal{K}}-\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,S\cup\mathcal{K}}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\bm{K}_{n}\begin{pmatrix}\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a,\mathcal{M}}-\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,\mathcal{M}}\\
\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a,S\cup\mathcal{K}}-\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,S\cup\mathcal{K}}\end{pmatrix}-(\bm{\omega_{n}}+\sqrt{n}\bm{h}_{n})^{T}{(\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T})^{-1}}(\bm{\omega_{n}}+\sqrt{n}\bm{h}_{n})\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~o_{p}\left((\bm{\omega_{n}}+\sqrt{n}\bm{h}_{n})^{T}{(\bm{C}\Omega_{mm}\bm{C}^{T})^{-1}}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}\right)+o_{p}(\bm{K}_{n})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~o_{p}(\sqrt{r}).

Along with equation (
118
) and the fact that
\mathbb{P}(\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{a,(\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K})^{c}}=\hat{\mathcal{B}}_{0,(\mathcal{M}\cup S\cup\mathcal{K})^{c}}=\bm{0})\rightarrow 1
proved in Theorem
1
, we can prove that

T_{L}=T_{0}+o_{p}(\sqrt{r})=T_{0}+o_{p}(r).

Step 4: Find the asymptotic distribution of
T_{0}
.

Define
\mathcal{T}_{n}=\text{Cov}(\bm{\omega}_{n})
, we then prove that

\sup_{\mathcal{A}}\left|\mathbb{P}\left(\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}\bm{\omega}_{n}\in\mathcal{A}\right)-\mathbb{P}\left(\bm{Z}\in\mathcal{A}\right)\right|\rightarrow 0,

(119)

where
\bm{Z}\in\mathbb{R}^{r}
stands for a mean zero Gaussian random vector with identity covariance matrix, and the supremum is taken over all convex sets
\mathcal{A}\subseteq\mathbb{R}^{r}
.
By the definition of
\bm{\omega}_{n}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}\bm{\omega}_{n}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{X}_{\mathcal{M}}^{T}\\
(\bm{X}^{k}_{S\cup\mathcal{K}})^{T}\end{pmatrix}\bm{H}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\left\{\bm{Y}^{k}-\bm{\mu}(\bm{X}^{k}{{\mathcal{B}^{*}}})\right\}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}h^{\prime}((\bm{x}_{i}^{k})^{T}{\mathcal{B}^{*}})\left\{\tilde{y}_{ik}-\mu((\bm{x}_{i}^{k})^{T}{\mathcal{B}^{*}})\right\}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{x}_{i,\mathcal{M}}\\
\bm{x}^{k}_{i,S\cup\mathcal{K}}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle=:\sum_{i=1}^{n}\xi_{i},

where
\xi_{i}
’s are independent. Since
\mathcal{T}_{n}=\text{Cov}(\bm{\omega}_{n})
, we have
\sum_{i=1}^{n}\text{Cov}(\xi_{i})=I_{r}
. By Theorem 1 in
Bentkus 2005
, we have

\sup_{\mathcal{A}}\left|\mathbb{P}\left(\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}\bm{\omega}_{n}\in\mathcal{A}\right)-\mathbb{P}\left(\bm{Z}\in\mathcal{A}\right)\right|\leq c_{0}r^{1/4}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\|\xi_{i}\|_{2}^{3},

for some constant
c_{0}
, where the supremum is taken for all convex subsets in
\mathbb{R}^{r}
. To bound
\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\|\xi_{i}\|_{2}^{3}
, notice that by convexity of
\|\cdot\|_{2}^{3}
,

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\|\xi_{i}\|_{2}^{3}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n^{3/2}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}h^{\prime}((\bm{x}_{i}^{k})^{T}{\mathcal{B}^{*}})\left\{\tilde{y}_{ik}-\mu((\bm{x}_{i}^{k})^{T}{\mathcal{B}^{*}})\right\}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{x}_{i,\mathcal{M}}\\
\bm{x}^{k}_{i,S\cup\mathcal{K}}\end{pmatrix}\right\|_{2}^{3}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n^{3/2}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\mathbb{E}\left\|\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}h^{\prime}((\bm{x}_{i}^{k})^{T}{\mathcal{B}^{*}})\left\{\tilde{y}_{ik}-\mu((\bm{x}_{i}^{k})^{T}{\mathcal{B}^{*}})\right\}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{x}_{i,\mathcal{M}}\\
\bm{x}^{k}_{i,S\cup\mathcal{K}}\end{pmatrix}\right\|_{2}^{3}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n^{3/2}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\left\|\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{x}_{i,\mathcal{M}}\\
\bm{x}^{k}_{i,S\cup\mathcal{K}}\end{pmatrix}\right\|_{2}^{3}\left|h^{\prime}((\bm{x}_{i}^{k})^{T}{\mathcal{B}^{*}})\right|^{3}\mathbb{E}\left|\tilde{y}_{ik}-\mu((\bm{x}_{i}^{k})^{T}{\mathcal{B}^{*}})\right|^{3}.

Since
\left|\tilde{y}_{ik}-\mu((\bm{x}_{i}^{k})^{T}{\mathcal{B}^{*}})\right|\leq 1
, we have
\mathbb{E}\left|\tilde{y}_{ik}-\mu((\bm{x}_{i}^{k})^{T}{\mathcal{B}^{*}})\right|^{3}=O(1)
.
By the 3rd condition of (A1) and
|h^{\prime\prime}|\leq 1
, we have
|h^{\prime}((\bm{x}_{i}^{k})^{T}{\mathcal{B}^{*}})|=O(1)
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\|\xi_{i}\|_{2}^{3}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n^{3/2}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\left\|\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{x}_{i,\mathcal{M}}\\
\bm{x}^{k}_{i,S\cup\mathcal{K}}\end{pmatrix}\right\|_{2}^{3}O(1)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n^{3/2}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\left\|\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}\bm{V}_{n}^{1/2}\right\|_{2}^{3}\left\|\bm{V}_{n}^{-1/2}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{x}_{i,\mathcal{M}}\\
\bm{x}^{k}_{i,S\cup\mathcal{K}}\end{pmatrix}\right\|_{2}^{3}O(1).

Notice that

\displaystyle\left\|\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}\bm{V}_{n}^{1/2}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\lambda_{\max}\left(\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}\bm{V}_{n}\bm{K}_{n}^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{C}^{T}\\
\bm{0}_{r\times(s+K)}^{T}\end{pmatrix}\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}\right)^{1/2}=1,

this along with the additional condition (
18
) in Theorem
2
:

\frac{r^{1/4}}{n^{3/2}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\left\{(\bm{x}_{i,\mathcal{M}\cup{S}\cup\mathcal{K}}^{k})^{T}\bm{V}_{n}^{-1}(\bm{x}_{i,\mathcal{M}\cup{S}\cup\mathcal{K}}^{k})\right\}^{3/2}\rightarrow 0,

implies that

\sup_{\mathcal{A}}\left|\mathbb{P}\left(\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}\bm{\omega}_{n}\in\mathcal{A}\right)-\mathbb{P}\left(\bm{Z}\in\mathcal{A}\right)\right|\rightarrow 0.

Since for any
x\in\mathbb{R}^{+}
, the event
\{T_{0}\leq x\}
is equivalent to the event
\{\mathcal{T}_{n}^{-1/2}\bm{\omega}_{n}\in\mathcal{A}_{x}\}
, where

\mathcal{A}_{x}=\left\{\bm{z}\in\mathbb{R}^{r}:\left\|\Psi_{n}^{-1/2}\left({\mathcal{T}_{n}}^{1/2}\cdot\bm{z}+\sqrt{n}\bm{h}_{n}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}\leq x\right\}.

It follows that

\sup_{x}\left|\mathbb{P}\left(T_{0}\leq x\right)-\mathbb{P}\left(\bm{Z}\in\mathcal{A}_{x}\right)\right|\rightarrow 0,

(120)

where

\left\|\Psi_{n}^{-1/2}\left({\mathcal{T}_{n}}^{1/2}\cdot\bm{Z}+\sqrt{n}\bm{h}_{n}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}
follows a generalized Chi-squared distribution.

Consider any statistic
T=T_{0}+o_{p}(r)
. For any
x
and any sufficiently small
\varepsilon>0
, we have
\mathbb{P}(|T-T_{0}|>\varepsilon r)=o(1)
. It follows from (
120
) that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}(T\leq x)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}(T_{0}\leq x+\varepsilon r)+\mathbb{P}(|T-T_{0}|>\varepsilon r)\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\bm{Z}\in\mathcal{A}_{x+\varepsilon r}\right)+o(1)

(121)

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}(T\leq x)

\displaystyle\geq\mathbb{P}(T_{0}\leq x-\varepsilon r)-\mathbb{P}(|T-T_{0}|>\varepsilon r)\geq\mathbb{P}\left(\bm{Z}\in\mathcal{A}_{x-\varepsilon r}\right)-o(1).

It remains to prove that

\lim_{\varepsilon\rightarrow 0^{+}}{\lim\sup}_{n}\left|\mathbb{P}\left(\bm{Z}\in\mathcal{A}_{x+\varepsilon r}\right)-\mathbb{P}\left(\bm{Z}\in\mathcal{A}_{x-\varepsilon r}\right)\right|=0

(122)

or equivalently,

\lim_{\varepsilon\rightarrow 0^{+}}{\lim\sup}_{n}\mathbb{P}\left(x-\varepsilon r\leq\left(\bm{Z}+\sqrt{n}{\mathcal{T}_{n}}^{-1/2}\bm{h}_{n}\right)^{T}{\mathcal{T}_{n}}^{1/2}\Psi_{n}^{-1}{\mathcal{T}_{n}}^{1/2}\left(\bm{Z}+\sqrt{n}{\mathcal{T}_{n}}^{-1/2}\bm{h}_{n}\right)\leq x+\varepsilon r\right)=0

This result can be proved using the fact that

\|\sqrt{n}{\mathcal{T}}_{n}^{-1/2}\bm{h}_{n}\|_{2}=O(\sqrt{r})

,

\lambda_{\max}({\mathcal{T}_{n}}^{1/2}\Psi_{n}^{-1}{\mathcal{T}_{n}}^{1/2})=O(1)

as well as

\lim\sup_{n}\lambda_{\min}({\mathcal{T}_{n}}^{1/2}\Psi_{n}^{-1}{\mathcal{T}_{n}}^{1/2})=O(1)

given in results (
47
), (
48
) and (
49
) of Lemma
2
. Combining (
121
) with (
122
), we obtain that

\sup_{x}\left|\mathbb{P}\left(T\leq x\right)-\mathbb{P}\left(\bm{Z}\in\mathcal{A}_{x}\right)\right|\rightarrow 0.

(123)

In the first three steps, we have shown
T_{0}=T_{W}+o_{p}(1)=T_{S}+o_{p}(1)=T_{L}+o_{p}(1)
. This together with (
123
) implies that the generalized
\chi^{2}
approximation holds for our partial penalized statistics. Hence, the proof is completed.

∎

A.4
Proof of Theorem
3

From the results
(
122
) proved in Theorem
2
, it suffices to prove that

\mathbb{P}\left(\bm{Z}^{T}{\mathcal{T}_{n}}^{1/2}\Psi_{n}^{-1}{\mathcal{T}_{n}}^{1/2}\bm{Z}\leq\hat{\chi}_{n,(1-\alpha)}|\bm{X},\bm{Y}\right)=1-\alpha+o_{p}(1),

(124)

For any fixed
\varepsilon>0
, we have the following inequality

\displaystyle~\mathbb{P}\left(\bm{Z}^{T}{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}{\widehat{\Psi}_{n,a}}^{-1}{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}\bm{Z}\leq\hat{\chi}_{n,(1-\alpha)}-\varepsilon|\bm{X},\bm{Y}\right)-\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\bm{Z}^{T}({\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}{\widehat{\Psi}_{n,a}}^{-1}{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}-{\mathcal{T}_{n}}^{1/2}\Psi_{n}^{-1}{\mathcal{T}_{n}}^{1/2})\bm{Z}\right|>\varepsilon|\bm{X},\bm{Y}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\mathbb{P}\left(\bm{Z}^{T}{\mathcal{T}_{n}}^{1/2}\Psi_{n}^{-1}{\mathcal{T}_{n}}^{1/2}\bm{Z}\leq\hat{\chi}_{n,(1-\alpha)}|\bm{X},\bm{Y}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\mathbb{P}\left(\bm{Z}^{T}{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}{\widehat{\Psi}_{n,a}}^{-1}{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}\bm{Z}\leq\hat{\chi}_{n,(1-\alpha)}+\varepsilon|\bm{X},\bm{Y}\right)+\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\bm{Z}^{T}({\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}{\widehat{\Psi}_{n,a}}^{-1}{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}-{\mathcal{T}_{n}}^{1/2}\Psi_{n}^{-1}{\mathcal{T}_{n}}^{1/2})\bm{Z}\right|>\varepsilon|\bm{X},\bm{Y}\right).

On the event

\mathcal{E}_{n}:=\left\{\left\|{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}{\widehat{\Psi}_{n,a}}^{-1}{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}-{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{1/2}{\Psi}_{n}^{-1}{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{1/2}\right\|_{2}\leq\frac{\varepsilon}{\frac{\varepsilon}{2}\frac{\sqrt{n}}{s+m+K}+r}\right\},

(125)

by the sub-exponential tail bound for
\chi^{2}(0,r)
, we have

\displaystyle~\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\bm{Z}^{T}({\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}{\widehat{\Psi}_{n,a}}^{-1}{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}-{\mathcal{T}_{n}}^{1/2}\Psi_{n}^{-1}{\mathcal{T}_{n}}^{1/2})\bm{Z}\right|>\varepsilon|\bm{X},\bm{Y}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\mathbb{P}\left(\left\|{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}{\widehat{\Psi}_{n,a}}^{-1}{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}-{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{1/2}{\Psi}_{n}^{-1}{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{1/2}\right\|_{2}\bm{Z}^{T}\bm{Z}>\varepsilon|\bm{X},\bm{Y}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\exp\left(-\frac{1}{8}\left(\frac{\varepsilon}{\|{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}{\widehat{\Psi}_{n,a}}^{-1}{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}-{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{1/2}{\Psi}_{n}^{-1}{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{1/2}\|_{2}}-r\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\exp\left(-\frac{\varepsilon}{16}\frac{\sqrt{n}}{s+m+K}\right),

which could be arbitrarily small as long as
n
is large enough. Furthermore, combining the fact that

\|{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}{\widehat{\Psi}_{n,a}}^{-1}{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}-{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{1/2}{\Psi}_{n}^{-1}{{\mathcal{T}}_{n}}^{1/2}\|_{2}=O_{p}\left({s+m+K}/{\sqrt{n}}\right)

proved in Lemma
2
as well as the additional condition
(s+m+K)r=o(\sqrt{n})
, we know that with probability tending to
1
, the event
\mathcal{E}_{n}
(
125
) holds. Therefore, the above ineuqality holds with probability tending to
1
. Then we have

\displaystyle~\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\bm{Z}^{T}({\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}{\widehat{\Psi}_{n,a}}^{-1}{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}-{\mathcal{T}_{n}}^{1/2}\Psi_{n}^{-1}{\mathcal{T}_{n}}^{1/2})\bm{Z}\right|>\varepsilon\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{1}_{\mathcal{E}_{n}}\cdot\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\bm{Z}^{T}({\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}{\widehat{\Psi}_{n,a}}^{-1}{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}-{\mathcal{T}_{n}}^{1/2}\Psi_{n}^{-1}{\mathcal{T}_{n}}^{1/2})\bm{Z}\right|>\varepsilon|\bm{X},\bm{Y}\right)\right]+\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}_{n}^{c})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\exp\left(-\frac{\varepsilon}{16}\frac{\sqrt{n}}{s+m+K}\right)+\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}_{n}^{c}),

which implies that

{\lim\sup}_{n}\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\bm{Z}^{T}({\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}{\widehat{\Psi}_{n,a}}^{-1}{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}-{\mathcal{T}_{n}}^{1/2}\Psi_{n}^{-1}{\mathcal{T}_{n}}^{1/2})\bm{Z}\right|>\varepsilon\right)=0.

(126)

Similar to the proof of (
122
) with
\bm{h}_{n}=\bm{0}
under the null hypothesis, we can prove that

\lim_{\varepsilon\rightarrow 0^{+}}{\lim\sup}_{n}\mathbb{P}\left(x-\varepsilon r\leq\bm{Z}^{T}{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}{\widehat{\Psi}_{n,a}}^{-1}{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}\bm{Z}\leq x+\varepsilon r|\bm{X},\bm{Y}\right)\overset{a.s.}{=}0.

Combining the previous results with the fact that

\mathbb{P}\left(\left.\bm{Z}^{T}{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}{\widehat{\Psi}_{n,a}}^{-1}{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}_{n,a}}^{1/2}\bm{Z}\leq\hat{\chi}_{n,(1-\alpha)}\right|\bm{X},\bm{Y}\right)=1-\alpha,

given by the definition of
\hat{\chi}_{n,(1-\alpha)}
, we proved the desired result (
124
).

A.5
Analysis of the Supermarket Data

In our empirical study, we use the supermarket data in
Lan et al. 2016
. This data set contains a total of
n=464
daily records. For each record, the response is the number of customers and the predictors are the sales volumes of
p=6398
products. Both the response and predictors are standardized so that they have zero mean and unit variance. This study identifies the products that attract the most customers and further performs hypothesis testings on those products to evaluate the impact of those products on the customers.

We apply the testing procedures of
Shi et al. 2019
under the standard normal linear model assumptions, and our testing procedures under non-parametric Box-Cox model to this dataset.
To formulate the testing hypotheses, we randomly split the data set into two subsets: one for preliminary analysis (
232
observations) and one for hypothesis testings (
232
observations).

A.6
Computing the partial penalized CPR estimators
\hat{\bm{\beta}}_{a}

From (
7
),
(\hat{\bm{\beta}}_{a},\hat{\bm{b}}_{a})
is the minimizer of the following objective function

{\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\left\{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}L(\check{y}_{ki}(\bm{x}_{i}^{T}\bm{\beta}-b_{k}))\right\}}+\sum_{j\notin\mathcal{M}}p_{\lambda}(|\beta_{j}|).

(127)

It can be solved by combining the local linear approximation (LLA) algorithm and the coordinate-majorization-descent (CMD) algorithm. Details of the LLA-CMD algorithm are discussed below.

Outer loop: local linear approximation

The local linear approximation (LLA) algorithm
Zou and Li, 2008
takes advantage of the special folded concave structure and utilizes the majorization-minimization (MM) principle to turn a concave regularization problem into a sequence of weighted
\ell_{1}
penalized problems.
Let
(\hat{\bm{\beta}}^{\textit{curr}},\hat{\bm{b}}^{\textit{curr}})
be the current estimate. The folded concave penalty could be majorized by a local linear approximation function:

\sum_{j\notin\mathcal{M}}p_{\lambda}(|\beta_{j}|)\leq\sum_{j\notin\mathcal{M}}p_{\lambda}(|\hat{\beta}_{j}^{\textit{curr}}|)+p_{\lambda}^{\prime}(|{\hat{\beta}_{j}^{\textit{curr}}}|)(|\beta_{j}|-|{\hat{\beta}_{j}^{\textit{curr}}}|),

(128)

which is the best convex majorization of the concave penalty function (Theorem 2 of
Zou and Li 2008
). Then the objective function of (
127
) could be majorized by a weighted
\ell_{1}
penalized problem:

M_{n}(\bm{\beta},\bm{b})+\sum_{j\notin\mathcal{M}}p_{\lambda}(|\hat{\beta}_{j}^{\textit{curr}}|)+p_{\lambda}^{\prime}(|{\hat{\beta}_{j}^{\textit{curr}}}|)(|\beta_{j}|-|{\hat{\beta}_{j}^{\textit{curr}}}|).

(129)

The details of the LLA algorithm are summarized in Algorithm
3
.

Inner loop: coordinate-majorization-descent

For our weighted
\ell_{1}
penalized composite probit regression problem (
136
) within each LLA iteration in Algorithm
3
, we may also apply the coordinate descent algorithm
Friedman et al., 2010
which has been successfully used in solving some high-dimensional models. In the case of probit regression, we need to pay attention to computer overflow errors that may occur during the computation of the CDF
\Phi(\cdot)
. Standard algorithms like Newton-Raphson are very sensitive to large values of the linear predictor
Demidenko, 2001
.

We prefer to use a numerically stable and efficient algorithm to solve (
136
). Due to the good property of probit regression loss given in Lemma 2 (refer to the first paper), that is, the second derivative of the probit regression loss function can be bounded by 1, we can fix the computer overflow error issue by using the coordinate-majorization-descent algorithm
Yang and Zou, 2013
which only uses the gradient information of the composite probit loss function.

Let
(\hat{\bm{\beta}}^{\textit{curr}},\hat{\bm{b}}^{\textit{curr}})
be the current estimate. Define the current margin
\hat{r}_{ki}^{\textit{curr}}=\check{y}_{ki}(\bm{x}_{i}^{T}{\hat{\bm{\beta}}^{\textit{curr}}}-\hat{{b}}_{k}^{\textit{curr}})
for
k=1,\cdots,K
,
i=1,\cdots,n
and current
\ell_{1}
weights

\hat{\omega}_{j}^{\textit{curr}}=p^{\prime}_{\lambda}(|\hat{\beta}^{\textit{curr}}_{j}|)
for
j\in\{1,\cdots,p\}\backslash\mathcal{M}
.

To update the
\zeta
-th coordinate of
\bm{\beta}
, where
\zeta\in\{1,\cdots,p\}\backslash\mathcal{M}
, define the
F
function:

F(\beta_{\zeta}|\hat{\bm{\beta}}^{\textit{curr}},\hat{\bm{b}}^{\textit{curr}}):=\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\left\{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}L(\check{y}_{ki}x_{i\zeta}(\beta_{\zeta}-{\hat{\beta}^{\textit{curr}}}_{\zeta})+\hat{r}_{ki}^{\textit{curr}})\right\}+\hat{\omega}^{\textit{curr}}_{\zeta}|\beta_{\zeta}|.

(130)

By Lemma
and
\check{y}_{ki}^{2}=1
, this
F
function can be majorized by a penalized quadratic function defined as

\mathcal{F}(\beta_{\zeta}|\hat{\bm{\beta}}^{\textit{curr}},\hat{\bm{b}}^{\textit{curr}}):=\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\cdot\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left[L(\hat{r}_{ki}^{\textit{curr}})+L^{\prime}(\hat{r}_{ki}^{\textit{curr}})\check{y}_{ki}x_{i\zeta}(\beta_{\zeta}-{\hat{\beta}^{\textit{curr}}}_{\zeta})+\frac{1}{2}x_{is}^{2}(\beta_{\zeta}-{\hat{\beta}^{\textit{curr}}}_{\zeta})^{2}\right]+\hat{\omega}^{\textit{curr}}_{\zeta}|\beta_{\zeta}|.

We can easily solve the minimizer of the majorization function by a simple soft thresholding rule:

{\hat{\beta}^{\textit{new}}}_{\zeta}=\mathcal{S}\left({\hat{\beta}^{\textit{curr}}}_{\zeta}+\frac{-\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\sum_{i=1}^{n}L^{\prime}(\hat{r}_{ki}^{\textit{curr}})\check{y}_{ki}x_{i\zeta}}{\sum_{i=1}^{n}x_{i\zeta}^{2}},\frac{\hat{\omega}^{\textit{curr}}_{\zeta}}{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}x_{i\zeta}^{2}}\right),

(131)

where
\mathcal{S}(z,t)=(|z|-t)_{+}\cdot\text{sgn}(z)
. The updating of the margins is given by

\hat{r}_{ki}^{\textit{new}}=\hat{r}_{ki}^{\textit{curr}}+\check{y}_{ki}x_{i\zeta}\cdot({\hat{\beta}^{\textit{new}}}_{\zeta}-{\hat{\beta}^{\textit{curr}}}_{\zeta}),~\text{for}~k=1,\dots,K,~i=1,\dots,n.

To update
\zeta
-th coordinate of
\bm{\beta}
, where
\zeta\in\mathcal{M}
, we follow the same trick and the updating rule is as follows:

\hat{\beta}_{\zeta}^{\textit{new}}={\hat{\beta}^{\textit{curr}}}_{\zeta}+\frac{-\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\sum_{i=1}^{n}L^{\prime}(\hat{r}_{ki}^{\textit{curr}})\check{y}_{ki}x_{i\zeta}}{\sum_{i=1}^{n}x_{i\zeta}^{2}}.

(132)

The updating of margins is also given by equation (
31
).

Simiarly, for the
\zeta
-th intercept
b_{\zeta}
, we consider minimizing the quadratic majorization:

\mathcal{F}(b_{\zeta}|\hat{\bm{\beta}}^{\textit{curr}},\hat{\bm{b}}^{\textit{curr}}):=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{L(\hat{r}_{{\zeta}i}^{\textit{curr}})+L^{\prime}(\hat{r}_{{\zeta}i}^{\textit{curr}})(-\check{y}_{{\zeta}i})(b_{\zeta}-\hat{b}_{\zeta}^{\textit{curr}})+\frac{1}{2}(b_{\zeta}-\hat{b}_{\zeta}^{\textit{curr}})^{2}\right\},

(133)

which has a minimizer

\hat{b}_{\zeta}^{\textit{new}}=\hat{b}_{\zeta}^{\textit{curr}}+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}L^{\prime}(r_{{\zeta}i})\check{y}_{{\zeta}i}.

(134)

The updating of margins is given by

\hat{r}_{ki}^{\textit{new}}=\begin{cases}\hat{r}_{\zeta i}^{\textit{curr}}-\check{y}_{{\zeta}i}(\hat{b}_{\zeta}^{\textit{new}}-\hat{b}_{\zeta}^{\textit{curr}}),&\text{if }k=\zeta;\\
\hat{r}_{ki}^{\textit{curr}},&\text{if }k\neq\zeta.\end{cases}

(135)

To sum up, the CMD algorithm for solving the weighted
\ell_{1}
-penalized CPR is given in Algorithm
4
.

Algorithm 3

The LLA Algorithm for solving unconstrained estimator

1:

Initialize
\hat{\bm{\beta}}^{(0)}=\hat{\bm{\beta}}^{\text{initial}}
and compute the adaptive weight

\hat{\bm{\omega}}^{(0)}=\left(\hat{\omega}_{j}^{(0)},~j\in\{1,\dots,p\}\backslash\mathcal{M}\right)=\left(p_{\lambda}^{\prime}(|\hat{\beta}_{j}^{(0)}|),~j\in\{1,\cdots,p\}\backslash\mathcal{M}\right)

2:

3

For
m=1,2,\cdots,
repeat the LLA iteration till convergence

(2.a)
4

Obtain
(\hat{\beta}^{(m)},\hat{\bm{b}}^{(m)})
by solving the following optimization problem

(\hat{\bm{\beta}}^{(m)},\hat{\bm{b}}^{(m)})=\arg\min_{\bm{\beta},\bm{b}}M_{n}(\bm{\beta},\bm{b})+\sum_{j\notin\mathcal{M}}\hat{\omega}_{j}^{(m-1)}\cdot|\beta_{j}|,

(136)

(2.b)
5

Update the adaptive weight vector
\hat{\bm{\omega}}^{(m)}
with
\hat{\omega}_{j}^{(m)}=p_{\lambda}^{\prime}(|\hat{\beta}_{j}^{(m)}|)
.

Algorithm 4

The CMD algorithm for solving (
136
).

1:

Input the weight vector
\hat{\bm{\omega}}
.

2:

Initialize
(\hat{\bm{\beta}},\hat{\bm{b}})
and its corresponding margin
\hat{r}_{ki}=\check{y}_{ki}(\bm{x}_{i}^{T}\hat{\bm{\beta}}-\hat{b}_{k})
, for
k=1,\dots,K
, and
i=1,\dots,n
.

3:

Iterate (3.a)-(3.b) until convergence:

(3.a)
3

Cyclic coordinate descent for coefficients: for
j=1,2,\cdots,p
,

(3.a.1)
4

Compute

\hat{\beta}_{j}^{\textit{new}}=\begin{cases}\mathcal{S}\left(\hat{\beta}_{j}+\frac{-\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\sum_{i=1}^{n}L^{\prime}(\hat{r}_{ki})\check{y}_{ki}x_{ij}}{\sum_{i=1}^{n}x_{ij}^{2}},\frac{\tilde{\omega}_{j}}{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}x_{ij}^{2}}\right),&\text{if }j\in\{1,\dots,p\}\backslash\mathcal{M};\\
\hat{\beta}_{j}+\frac{-\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\sum_{i=1}^{n}L^{\prime}(\hat{r}_{ki})\check{y}_{ki}x_{ij}}{\sum_{i=1}^{n}x_{ij}^{2}},&\text{if }j\in\mathcal{M}.\end{cases}

(3.a.2)
5

Compute the new margins:
\hat{r}_{ki}^{\textit{new}}=\hat{r}_{ki}+\check{y}_{ki}x_{ij}\cdot({\hat{\beta}^{\textit{new}}}_{j}-\hat{\beta}_{j})
,
for
k=1,\dots,K
,
i=1,\dots,n
.

(3.a.3)
6

Update
\hat{\beta}_{j}={\hat{\beta}_{j}}^{\textit{new}}
, and
\hat{r}_{ki}=\hat{r}_{ki}^{\textit{new}}
, for
k=1,\dots,K
,
i=1,\dots,n
.

(3.b)
7

Cyclic coordinate descent for intercepts: for
k=1,2,\cdots,K
,

(3.b.1)
8

Compute

\hat{b}_{k}^{\textit{new}}=\hat{b}_{k}+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}L^{\prime}(r_{ki})\check{y}_{ki}.

(3.b.2)
9

Compute the new margins:
\hat{r}_{ki}^{\textit{new}}=\hat{r}_{ki}-\check{y}_{ki}(\hat{b}_{k}^{\textit{new}}-\hat{b}_{k}),
for
i=1,\dots,n
.

(3.b.3)
10

Update
\hat{b}_{k}=\hat{b}_{k}^{\textit{new}}
, and
\hat{r}_{ki}=\hat{r}_{ki}^{\textit{new}}
, for
i=1,\dots,n
.
